## Document 001: 10月PMI数据点评：节前需求释放后回调，增量订单仍待接续

Source PDF: all report pdf/10月PMI数据点评：节前需求释放后回调，增量订单仍待接续.pdf
Report source: 联储证券
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

证券研究报告 
宏观经济点评 2026 年 11 月 03 日

请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明

10 月 PMI 数据点评：节前需求释放后回调，增量
订单仍待接续

[Table_Author] 
 魏争 分析师  沈夏宜 分析师

Email:weizheng@lczq.com Email:shenxiayi@lczq.com  
证书:S1320524100001 证书:S1320523020004

### Page 2

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 2 
购进价格指数下降 4.0 个百分点至 56.8%，出厂价格指数下降 2.3 个百分
点至 51.7%，均保持在扩张区间，指向工业品价格仍在上涨，上涨的扩散
程度较 9 月减弱。两项指数之差由 6.8 个百分点收窄至 5.1 个百分点，表
明购进端相对出厂端的价格上涨压力边际缓和。国家统计局指出，受国际
原油价格持续攀升影响，石油煤炭及其他燃料加工、化学纤维及橡胶塑料
制品等行业的购进和出厂价格指数均继续处于 60%以上高位1。在订单走
弱的背景下，高位能源价格仍可能强化部分企业控制采购、谨慎排产的倾
向。 
中小企业和消费品行业景气回调，装备订单仍有局部支撑。从企业规模
看，大型企业仍保持扩张，中小企业则回吐了 9 月的改善。10 月，大、
中、小型企业 PMI 分别下降 0.4、1.3 和 1.4 个百分点至 50.2%、48.4%
和 47.5%。大型与小型企业景气差距由 1.7 个百分点扩大至 2.7 个百分
点。从行业看，高技术制造业和装备制造业 PMI 分别下降 1.2 和 0.4 个百
分点至 51.3%和 50.6%，仍处于扩张区间，其中，装备制造业新订单指数
较上月上升 1 个百分点至 51%以上，或与“六张网”逐步推进形成实物工
作量有关；消费品行业 PMI 下降 1.9 个百分点至 48.8%，高耗能行业下降
0.7 个百分点至 47.3%。 
服务业景气小幅回落，假日消费形成支撑，新增需求仍显不足。 10 月，
服务业商务活动指数下降 0.2 个百分点至 50.0%，回到荣枯线。细分看，
一是行业表现分化，假日消费与信息服务仍有支撑。铁路运输、住宿、文
化体育娱乐等行业商务活动指数明显回升，邮政、互联网软件及信息技术
服务等行业保持在 55%以上；金融行业景气回落较大，是服务业总指数下
降的主要拖累。二是新增订单与销售价格同步转弱。新订单指数下降 1.5
个百分点至 45.2%，销售价格指数下降 1.0 个百分点至 49.6%，重新低于
荣枯线，指向新增需求承接及企业定价能力仍然偏弱。 三是用工景气边际
改善，但经营预期略有回落。从业人员指数上升 0.5 个百分点至 46.7%，
仍处于收缩区间；业务活动预期指数下降 0.3 个百分点至 55.2%，指向企
业对后续经营仍保持一定信心。 
建筑业景气重新回落，新增订单明显走弱，施工需求仍待释放。 10 月，
建筑业商务活动指数下降 2.3 个百分点至 48.0%，重返收缩区间。一是施
工活动放缓，内部表现分化。房屋建筑业商务活动指数明显回落至 50%以
下，土木工程建筑业指数回升，但仍低于荣枯线，指向部分基建施工边际
改善。二是新增订单明显收缩，后续施工需求承压。新订单指数下降 5.7
个百分点至 40.0%，显示新增项目需求景气进一步走弱，当前施工放缓 可
能并不只是既有项目建设节奏较慢的影响。三是用工与经营预期同步回
落。从业人员指数下降 2.3 个百分点至 42.0%，业务活动预期指数下降
3.1 个百分点至 52.3%，企业用工需求偏弱，对后续经营的预期也趋于谨
慎。 
短期回调能否修复，取决于新增订单能否接续。 10 月 PMI 边际下滑主因
节前集中生产和备货结束后，生产、订单、采购同步回调， 同时优势行业

### Page 3

宏观经济点评  
请务必阅读正文之后的信息披露和法律声明 3 
的局部扩张不足以带动制造业整体保持景气。往后看，一是需要观察新订
单与在手订单能否同步回升，区分短期节奏调整与需求持续转弱， 同时我
们也不建议仅以生产指数反弹确认修复；二是观察能源价格压力能否缓
解，以及价格指数差值收窄能否转化为中下游利润改善； 三是观察政策需
求能否由装备采购进一步传导至施工和传统原材料行业。截至 10 月 28
日，8000 亿元新型政策性金融工具已全部完成投放；财政部安排的 2500
亿元专项债务结存限额，也优先保障在建项目并支持“六张网”等重点领
域，为后续投资提供资金条件。但资本金投放、配套融资、项目开工到形
成实物工作量可能也会存在时滞。

风险提示：外需订单持续回落；能源价格进一步上涨并挤压中下游利润；
财政金融资金形成实物工作量不及预期；节前需求释放后的消费与补货接
续偏弱。

投资评级说明 
投资建议的评级标准  评级 说明 
评级标准为报告发布日后的 6 个月内公司股价 （或行业
指数） 相对同期基准指数的相对市场表现。 其中A 股市
场以沪深 300 指数为基准； 新三板市场以三板成指 （针
对协议转让标的） 或三板做市指数 （针对做市转让标的）
为基准；香港市场以恒生指数为基准。 
股票评级 
买入 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 10%以上 
增持 相对同期基准指数涨幅在 5%~10%之间 
中性 相对同期基准指数涨幅在-5%~+5%之间 
减持 相对同期基准指数跌幅在 5%以上 
行业评级 
看好 相对表现优于市场 
中性 相对表现与市场持平 
看淡 相对表现弱于市场

市

顧
市場焦點
美國
科技股
• 美國企業獲利維持強勁 ，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及 AI資本支出熱潮支撐 。從獲利來看 ，市
場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，
雖呈現逐步降溫 ，但仍維持雙位數增長 。標普500預期本益比已由約 23倍降至19倍，且並非
主要來自股價修正 ，而是企業獲利預期上調所致 ，在企業獲利維持成長 、AI投資持續挹注及
估值已較高點收斂的情況下，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。
美債
外匯對沖視角
• 在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下 ，美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢 ，今年以美元計價總
回報優於多數G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率落底訊號以重返市場 ，
對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言 ，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位 ，具備

相當良好的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。
台股
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈，綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡
的紅燈，連續第9個月亮出紅燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。
• 根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成 ，除了AI投資趨勢未減 ，年底集團作帳行情以
及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒 。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表
現，有望上看53,000點。此外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。

就業增幅明顯放緩，降低Fed短期再升息急迫性
• 非農明顯降溫，勞動市場尚未出現全面惡化：9月非農僅增2.9萬人，遠低於預期，且7、8月合計下修6萬人；失業率升
至4.2%，但勞參率同步升至 61.8%，家戶調查就業增加 40.6萬人，顯示失業率上升部分來自勞動供給增加 ，其中營建、
製造業仍分別增加1.1萬及0.9萬人，平均工時維持34.4小時，數據呈現招聘動能明顯放慢，但尚未出現全面性就業惡化。
• 薪資增速放緩，Fed短期政策壓力下降：9月平均時薪月增僅0.1%、年增3.0%，均低於前值；同時8月PCE年增3.4%、
核心PCE年增3.0%，顯示薪資通膨壓力有所緩和 ，但整體物價仍高於Fed 2%目標，在9月Fed升息1碼後，非農報告降
低Fed在10月連續升息的必要性，市場焦點轉向年底通膨與AI投資驅動的產業動能。

• 投資建議：短期關注美國中短天期投資級債券 ，就業降溫與薪資放緩有助降低短端利率上行壓力 ，但通膨仍高於目標 ，
因此不宜過度 預期長天期利率快速回落；股市則可續關注 AI基礎建設與資本支出相關產業 ，因營建及製造業就業仍具
韌性，顯示企業投資動能尚未明顯轉弱。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics， 2026.10.3

消費動能回升、通膨低於預期，內需韌性與物價壓力並存
• PCE低於預期、通膨降溫仍待確認：美國8月PCE物價指數月增0.3%、年增3.4%，核心PCE月增0.2%、年增3.0%，均低於
市場預期；其中核心PCE較修訂後7月的3.3%下降，顯示物價壓力有所緩解，但仍高於Fed 2%目標，此次年度更新回溯修
訂2021年以來資料，亦使歷史基期重新調整，後續仍須觀察通膨下降是否能延續。
• 消費回升、所得增幅偏緩：8月個人所得月增0.2%，個人消費支出（PCE）月增0.9%、實質消費成長0.6%，顯示內需仍具
韌性；惟消費增幅明顯高於所得 ，個人儲蓄率由7月4.6%降至4.1%，為2022年11月以來低點，反映家庭可支應消費的財
務緩衝持續縮小，後續消費動能仍須觀察。
• 後市觀察：PCE低於預期後，市場對10月再升息的預期明顯降溫，但長端美債殖利率仍受通膨、財政供給與期限溢價牽動

而維持高檔；建議可聚焦獲利成長與現金流穩健的優質大型成長股與短天期美債以掌握收益 ，同時留意儲蓄率下滑與 9月
消費者信心低迷可能抑制後續消費動能，動態調整部位並嚴控風險。
資料來源：U.S. Bureau of Economic Analysis， 2026.10.1

• 長端殖利率上行 、期限溢價成重要推力 ：近期
美債呈現短端與長端走勢分化 ，雖市場對 10月
Fed再升息預期因 8月PCE低於預期而明顯降溫 ，
10年期及30年期的美債殖利率仍偏高位 ，反映
通膨風險 、財政融資需求與期限溢價上升 ，長
天期無風險利率維持高檔 ，並推升房貸及企業
融資成本。
• 能源與AI投資推升通膨及資金需求 ：油價維持
高檔增加通膨黏著風險 ，AI基礎建設投資則帶
動企業資本支出與融資需求 ，兩者共同提高長
期資金需求；在財政赤字與美債供給仍大的環
境下，長端殖利率可能維持較高水準 ，進而提
高股票估值折現率及高槓桿企業的融資壓力。
• 後市觀察： 短期持續觀察油價 、10年及30年期
美債殖利率與期限溢價變化；債券配置仍建議

降低長天期債券部位以降低利率風險 ，優先布
局短天期高品質債券獲取穩健息收 ，股票則聚
焦具穩健現金流 、低財務槓桿與定價能力的企
業，以降低高利率環境下的估值與融資風險。

美光最新一季財報與財測 2026會計年度Q4實際 市場預期 2027會計年度Q1財測

AI需求大增，美光財測超過市場預期
• AI記憶體需求強勁，美光財測顯示景氣延續：美光科技9/30盤後公布財報，2026財年的第四季營收達542.3億美元，年增

379%，調整後EPS 33.42美元，均優於市場預期；公司並預估2027會計年度第一季營收615億美元、調整後EPS 38.15美元，
分別高於市場預期的567.7億美元及36.02美元。強勁財測反映AI資料中心持續推升HBM、伺服器DRAM及高速SSD需求。
隨Amazon、Alphabet等大型科技企業持續擴大AI基礎建設投資，記憶體需求仍具成長動能。
• 長約與產品升級，支撐中長期業績：美光目前已簽署36份多年期供應協議，涵蓋約三分之一營收並延伸至2030年，有助降
低記憶體產業週期波動；同時持續布局HBM4、HBM4E及高容量DDR5等產品。公司2026會計年度自由現金流達590億美元，
並預期2027、2028會計年度供需將進一步緊俏。整體而言，AI需求已由單一HBM擴展至伺服器記憶體與儲存產品，美光財
測亦顯示景氣延續性仍強，後續市場焦點將轉向擴產速度、資本支出及AI需求成長能否維持。
資料來源：美光，彭博，鉅亨網，2026.10.1

• AI驅動獲利成長，企業基本面仍具支撐：美國企業獲利維持強勁，背後主要受經濟展望穩健及AI資本支出熱潮支撐。從獲利
來看，市場共識預期標普500企業EPS成長率2026年達28%，2027年及2028年則分別為20%及16%，雖呈現逐步降溫，但
仍維持雙位數增長。其中，AI基礎建設與大型雲端業者仍是重要驅動來源，2026年對EPS成長的貢獻合計約19%，2027、
2028年仍分別貢獻14%及11%。
• 獲利上修降低估值壓力：標普500預期本益比已由約23倍降至19倍，且並非主要來自股價修正，而是企業獲利預期上調所致。
雖然未來EPS成長率若由高檔回落，市場仍可能面臨評價修正壓力，但在企業獲利維持成長、AI投資持續挹注及估值已較高
點收斂的情況下，標普500基本面仍具支撐。因此，短期應留意獲利增速放緩與市場持倉偏高所帶來的波動，但從獲利成長

與估值兩大面向觀察，標普500仍具中長期配置價值。

BCI:企業利潤前瞻指數 BCI:企業銷售前瞻指數
2026年製造業PMI 2024年 2025年 2023年
生產回升與價格上漲同步推動中國PMI改善
• 生產回升與價格上漲同步推動 中國PMI改善： 9月製造業PMI升至50.1%，較前月回升0.3個百分點，重返榮枯線以上，顯示
製造業景氣有所改善 ，主要受生產活動回暖及價格上漲帶動 ，中小企業景氣同步改善 ，但仍低於榮枯線，大型企業表現相對
較佳，高技術製造、裝備製造維持擴張 ，消費品PMI升至50.7%，重回景氣區間 ，另一方面，原材料及能源價格上漲推升企
業成本，上下游獲利差距持續擴大 。 非製造業PMI則由49.0%升至50.2%，服務業受節慶效應帶動回溫 ，建築業則受財政資

金投入，及基建建設推進而改善，不過需求復甦仍偏溫和，企業成本壓力尚未明顯減輕。
• 結論：第四季隨著基建投資、財政支出及房市支持政策逐步落地，經濟可望獲得支撐，但10月受長假因素影響，PMI可能短
期承壓。

台灣景氣燈號連9紅 台股Q4有望突破5萬點大關
• 8月景氣對策燈號續亮紅燈： 綜合判斷分數41分，較前月持平，對應的燈號仍是代表景氣熱絡的紅燈 ，連續第9個月亮出紅
燈，追平史上最長「連紅」紀錄，顯示國內經濟維持成長。國發會表示，AI需求令台廠積極擴廠，加上政府推動AI新十大建
設，民間投資有望維持動能。此外，受惠財富效應、企業調薪及就業市場穩定，民間消費有望持續成長。
• 法人看好台股年底前站上5萬點：根據歷史統計第四季台股上漲的機率接近八成 ，除了AI投資趨勢未減，年底集團作帳行情
以及美國11月期中選後不確定性下降有望支撐台股多頭情緒 。群益投顧樂觀看待台股第四季表現 ，有望上看53,000點。此
外，彭博綜合統計台灣加權指數12個月目標價達59,688點。
• 觀點：近期台股站上48,000點整數關卡，總市值達5.3兆美元，讓台股耀升成為全球第四大市值股市 ，顯示台股在AI浪潮下

的得天獨厚。年底前仍須留意中東衝突進展，若能源通膨壓力持續，聯準會升息預期恐為股市帶來波動，但考慮AI科技持續
發酵，建議投資人續抱台股，並以定期不定額的策略紀律加碼，目標追求中長期完整的AI趨勢回報。
資料來源：國發會，2026.09.29。

外匯對沖視角—美債對沖後殖利率屬全球最低之一
• 美國國債殖利率真的高嗎？在當前全球總體經濟環境下，美國國債殖利率雖維持在數十年歷史高點附近，但若單憑名目殖利
率來判斷美債的吸引力，容易忽視匯率對沖與各國財政風險帶來的溢價差異。
• 美元視角下的殖利率比較： 若將其他已開發市場的主權債務 (如德債、日債、英債)完全對沖回美元，美債在殖利率曲線上多
數期限的實際殖利率，其實與德債並列全球最低水準。相反地，日本國債與英國國債經匯率對沖回美元後的收益率反而更高。
• 跨貨幣資產交換 (ASW)的真實回報： 以10年期主權債的對沖跨貨幣資產交換計算：美國 10年期國債有效殖利率約 4.85%，
日本10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約5.06%，德國10年期國債(對沖至美元)有效殖利率約4.51%，這表明美國政府發

債的真實對沖融資成本其實已相對精實，且符合國際市場對各國財政基本面的合理定價。

財政基本面與信用風險—殖利率溢價反映國家債務比重
• 從歐元計價或信用風險的角度來比較 ，美債的定價邏輯非常符合總體經濟基本面： 儘管債務以歐元計價時 ，10年期美債殖
利率比同期限德國國債高出約30個基點，這反映出德國的債務佔GDP比重僅約65%，而美國則高達127%，因此美債承擔了
適度的財政溢價。儘管義大利的債務佔GDP比重高達140%且信用前景較遜色，但在統一貨幣基準下，義大利國債殖利率仍
較美國國債高出60個基點，這顯示市場並未過度壓低美債信用，美債依然享有優質避險資產的溢價優勢。
• 海外私人投資者的資金流向：近一年海外私人機構在美債加碼速度放緩，乃通膨環境下全球其他資產選擇增加，及過去升息
波段導致帳面按市值計價虧損 。然而歐洲投資人(持有一半以上的海外美債)目前仍持有龐大部位，並在等待適當的加碼時機。

• 小結與投資建議：在經過外匯對沖後，美債收益率在發達國家主權債中處於相對低位，發行成本合理且具競爭力。美債殖利
率高於德債低於義債 ，精確地反映美國政府債務負擔率 (127%)與總體經濟穩健度 。在全球公債遭遇拋售潮與通膨陰影下 ，
美債因利率期間結構與匯率優勢 ，今年以美元計價總回報優於多數 G7主權債，儘管海外私人機構投資人正在等待長端利率
落底訊號以重返市場，對美元資金或以美元計價為主的投資人而言，當前美債名目殖利率仍處於數十年高位，具備相當良好
的長期鎖利價值，但價格波動風險考量下，建議債券佈局仍以中短天期為主。

亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健
• 亞洲晶⽚債券跑輸指數，評級保持穩健：儘管AI熱潮推動收⼊創下紀錄 ，但SK海力⼠和台積電的美元債 ，今年以來的表現仍
略遜於彭博亞洲美元信用債總回報指數 ，主因為大規模資本支出擴張 ，以及潛在的投⼊資本回報率下降令 其利差承壓，美國
雲服務巨頭創紀錄的 AI支出計畫加劇了壓力 ，儘管如此 SK 海力⼠和台積電仍保持較強的高評級信用實力 ，台積電計畫投
⼊600-640億美元資本支出 ，SK海力⼠的資本支出則處於 40兆韓圜區間。兩家公司穩健的資產負債表 、合計共1,260億美元
的淨現金、強勁的經營性現金流，以及在先進製程，和HBM晶⽚領域的市場領先地位，為上述支出提供支撐。
• 投資建議：現階段台積電與SK海力⼠美元債最大的問題不是信用風險 ，而是估值與利差壓力 ，短期可能因AI資本支出過大，

及市場情緒波動而表現落後指數 ，但以兩家公司強大的現金流 、淨現金部位及產業領導地位來看 ，信用品質仍屬亞洲投資級
債中的優質標的，建議利差擴大時分批布局 。

顧 • 企業獲利成長將支撐股價上行：標普500指數的長期走勢與企業每股盈餘（EPS）高度連動。高盛預估，標普500企業EPS將
於2026年達375美元，並在2027年進一步升至415美元，對應年增率約36%與11%。在利率維持高檔、金融條件偏緊的環境
下，企業獲利仍持續處於成長軌道，將為股市提供穩健的中期上行動能。
• 本益比回落提供潛在布局契機：根據FactSet數據，標普500企業第三季整體獲利預計成長30.6%。其中，資訊科技類股獲利
成長預估高達66.5%，領先能源以外的其他產業；同時，該類股預估本益比已由今年高點回落，在獲利上修與估值修正同步
發生的背景下，浮現具吸引力的中期布局機會。
• 小結：短期市場仍可能受地緣政治與利率預期擾動，但中期趨勢仍由企業獲利擴張所主導。建議投資人採取分批布局科技股

策略，聚焦具備AI受益結構、資本支出能見度高及現金流穩健的龍頭企業。

2. 本資料提及之經濟走勢預測不必然代表基金之績效，基金投資風險請詳閱基金公開說明書。
3. 基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良管理人之注意義務外，不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益 ，投資人申購
前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
4. 本資料所載內容如涉及商品資訊 ，商品相關資訊仍以經核可之條款及文宣 (DM)為主，本行網站已備有公開說明書可供查閱基金應負擔之費用及相關投資風險 。投
資人申購基金係持有基金受益憑證，而非本資料提及之投資資產或標的。
5. 本資料所載內容取自本公司相信可靠之資料來源，且為特定日期之判斷，本公司不保證其即時性、精確性及完整性，亦不提供或嘗試遊說客戶做為投資之判斷依據。

資料內容邇後若有變更，本公司將不做預告或主動更新。投資人於決策時應審慎衡量本身風險，如因信賴本資料內容而做出或改變投資決策，須自行承擔結果，本
公司及本公司所屬員工，均不負任何損害賠償及其他法律上之責任。
6. 本資料內容之著作權屬兆豐銀行所有，禁止就所載資料任何形式之抄襲、引用或轉載。
7. 本資料所載部分基金涉及非投資等級債，投資人投資以非投資等級債券為訴求之基金不宜占其投資組合過高之比重 。基金經金管會核准，惟不表示絕無風險。由於
非投資等級債券之信用評等未達投資等級或未經信用評等，且對利率變動的敏感度甚高，故本基金可能會因利率上升、市場流動性下降，或債券發行機構違約不支
付本金、利息或破產而蒙受虧損。部分基金不適合無法承擔相關風險之投資人。基金經理公司以往之經理績效不保證基金之最低投資收益；基金經理公司除盡善良
管理人之注意義務外不負責本基金之盈虧，亦不保證最低之收益，投資人申購前應詳閱基金公開說明書。
8. 基金年化配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除息日前一日之淨值×一年配息次數×100%。「年化配息率」為估算值。當月配息率計算公式：每單位配息金額 ÷除

息日前一日之淨值×100%。
9. 基金配息率不代表基金報酬率，且過去配息率不代表未來配息率；基金淨值可能因市場因素而上下波動。投資人因不同時間進場，將有不同之投資績效，過去之績
效亦不代表未來績效之保證。本資料如以基金過去績效進行模擬投資組合之報酬率時，僅為歷史資料模擬投資組合之結果，不代表本投資組合之實際報酬率及未來
績效保證，不同時間進行模擬操作，其結果亦可能不同。
10. 部分基金配息前未先扣除應負擔之相關費用。基金的配息可能由基金的收益或本金中支付。任何涉及由本金支出的部份，可能導致原始投資金額減損。各基金由本
金支付配息之相關資料，投資人可至各投信基金公司及總代理人網站查詢。
11. 屬基金資訊觀測站ESG基金專區所列之基金，有關基金之ESG資訊，投資人應於申購前詳閱基金公開說明書或投資人須知所載之基金所有特色或目標等資訊。

## Document 121: 261030_daiwa_MTK

Source PDF: all report pdf/261030_daiwa_MTK.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: MediaTek (MTK) disclosed on 30 October a strong set of 
quarterly results and guidance both beating market expectations, thanks to 
a faster-than-expected ramp in its first AI accelerator project. We walked 
away from today’s call with a firmer view on its AI business - a faster ramp 
for the first project and a broadened customer base for a new project. We 
raise our forecasts and 12M TP, reiterate our Buy (1) call and suggest that 
investors take advantage of the recent stock pullbacks for bargain-hunting.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. MTK released 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD28bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 11%/9% 
thanks to a better topline, lower opex ratio and higher non-operating gains 
(table on p.2). 3Q26 revenue finished 6%/4% above our/consensus 
estimates and topped guidance (TWD152-160bn), thanks to an earlier 
ramp of its first AI accelerators on some easing of the substrate supply 
constraint. Guidance calls for 4Q26 revenue of TWD216bn, at the midpoint 
of the range that beat our previous/consensus forecasts by 7%/3%, driven 
by the stronger ramp of the AI accelerator project which will more than 
offset muted consumer demand weakness associated with mobile phones. 
We raise our 2026-28E EPS by 1-6% and expect its AI revenue to reach 
USD16bn in 2027 and USD26bn in 2028, from USD2.7bn this year.

AI appears to be running ahead. At today’s call, management centred 
elaboration on its AI business progress. 1) It raised this year’s initial 
revenue contribution of the first AI accelerators to USD2.5bn for 4Q26, 
above its previous target of USD2bn. 2) While management keeps its AI 
accelerator (ex-HBM) addressable market unchanged at USD80bn for 
2027, it now aims for a 20% market share (previous: 15-20%), meaning 
USD16bn revenue contribution, vs. our previous forecast of USD15bn. 3) It 
expects revenue contribution in 2028 from the second AI customer (not 
from China), which supports our view depicted in our Global Tech report 
issued on 27 August, where we lifted MTK’s 2028 forecasts on the second 
customer ramp. 4) MTK aims to kick off 4Q26 with a new journey for its 
operating margin, expanding all the way into 2027 for more than 20% on 
average, echoing its previous comments that, although the AI accelerator 
business is gross-margin dilutive, it is operating-margin accretive.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we finetune our 12M 
TP for MTK to TWD5,500 (previous: TWD5,200), based on our unchanged 
4-quarter forward PER of 36x. We reiterate our Buy (1) rating with key 
downside risk of any AI accelerator ramps below our expectation.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 6-8% above the consensus 
forecasts as we believe the street has yet to factor in its better AI ramp.

30 October 2026

Firmly on track with AI ASIC transformer

Company profile 
Ranked No.5 in the world by 2023 fabless semiconductor revenue, MediaTek (MTK) offers diverse 
products across optical storage, digital home, smartphone, PC, ASIC, automotive and other IoT 
applications. MTK has successfully captured the industry demand cycles from optical disk drives to 
feature phones to smartphones, and now positions itself well as a platform provider to capitalise on the 
next demand cycle of the BigData/AI to help facilitate digital transformation in human lives.

### Page 5

5

MediaTek (2454 TT): 30 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 4 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
MTK’s Board of Directors (BoD) has 8 members with diverse backgrounds in foundry, IC design
and electronics. Among its BoD, 4 members are independent directors, representing 50% of total
directors. Its Chairman and CEO are not the same person, but its CEO is also one of the
directors. MTK conducts internal annual performance evaluation of the BoD, individual directors
and functional committees.
The board currently includes only one female director out of eight members, which represents
12.5% of the total. The company recognizes this gender imbalance and is actively working
toward a more inclusive and balanced board composition
Capital management 2
MTK’s dividend payout ratios in the past 5 years were above 60% with increasing cash dividend
in the past 3 years in dollar amount. In addition, MTK is committed to paying a special dividend
of TWD16/share over 2021-24 on top of its regular annual dividend. MTK is one of the leading
fabless chipmakers in the world with continued efforts in new product development for diversified
applications; therefore, it will continue to invest in its research & development. We see its
dividend payout as appropriate.
Related party &
transaction 1
MTK’s revenue from related parties was less than 1% of its total revenue in 2024 and transaction
terms were not abnormal compared to other customers. It also had related-party transactions
with King Yuan Electronics Co., Ltd. and King Long Tech (Suzhou) Ltd for IC testing,
experimental services, and manufacturing technology services in 2021. The purchase amount
from related parties was less than 7% of its total cost of goods sold/purchases in 2024. Thus, we
do not see meaningful impact on its business operation.
S Supply chain management 2
MTK has established its "Supplier Code of Conduct", which covers areas such as labour and
human rights, health and safety, environmental protection, code of ethics, and management. It
requires all suppliers to sign a guarantee to comply with this code of conduct. MTK implements
an annual review to continuously track the actual compliance of suppliers. We believe these
measures can help MTK reduce supply chain risk.
E Water & wastewater management 1
As a fabless without manufacturing plants, MTK does not produce much wastewater compared
to manufacturing companies, and thus has relatively limited impact on the environment.
MTK has initiated water conservation methods, including efficient enhancement of using water-
saving apparatuses, installing rainwater recycling tanks and using recycled water in cooling
towers.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
MTK prioritises waste reduction, classification for reuse, adherence to recycling, and reutilisation
to manage waste efficiently and maximise the benefits of recycling. Also, it chooses only
qualified partners for waste disposal and recycling, and also audits the waste processes
regularly to ensure legal disposal of its waste and to fulfil its responsibility in waste management
supervision.
E GHG emissions 1
The main source for MTK’s GHG emissions is externally purchased electricity, which accounts
for over 90% of its total emissions. To enhance energy efficiency and reduce emissions, the
company implemented several measures, including ongoing improvements to data center
efficiency, full adoption of LED lighting in offices, and optimized operation of chiller units.
MediaTek continued advancing its renewable energy strategy and alignment with global climate
initiatives such as SBT and RE100. In early 2025, the Company passed the SBTi review and set
targets based on a 2020 baseline, including a 40% reduction in Scope 1 and 2 emissions by
2030, net zero by 2050, and achieving 100% renewable energy use across global offices
(excluding data centers) by 2030.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

營收走勢圖

研究員/鄭高輯 OWENCHENG@uni-psg.com

結論: 
環球晶短期獲利表現低於預期，主要原因包括: (1) 義大利 Novara
廠 7 月火災影響、(2)短期產能受限，以及(3)2H26 仍適用舊LTA 價
格 。 不過最壞狀況已過 ， 隨著時序進入4Q26， 預期營收將優於3Q26，
且自 1Q27 起 ， 隨著新產能開出 ， 以及開始適用價格調漲後的新LTA，
獲利表現將明顯轉佳 。 預估2026、2027 年稅後 EPS 分別為17.82 元
(原估 19.49 元)、33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。在投資建議上，儘管
短期營運不如預期 ， 但環球晶股價已大幅修正 ， 且2027 年半導體及
記憶體新 Fab 產能開出的趨勢明確(13 座->23 座)， 因此矽晶圓供需
轉緊且價格上漲的趨勢不變， 故維持買進建議 ， 然考量近期AI 供應
鏈出貨不順的雜音較多 ，漲價概念股評價受壓抑 ， 故將PBR 由 6X 調
整至 5X (PBR 歷史區間 2~6X，漲價循環合理評價應在中上緣 4~6X
之間)，將目標價由 1350 元下調至 1170 元。

重點摘要:

1.3Q26 財報表現不佳，主要受義大利 Novara 廠火災影響:  
3Q26 營收-2.3%QoQ(+2.6%YoY)，若排除 7 月義大利 Novara 廠火災
之影響，營收原本可以成長雙位數%QoQ。在毛利率方面，儘管3Q26
折舊費用略較前季下降 ， 但因Novara 火災導致部分高毛利率的 8 吋
SOI 基板出貨中斷 ， 導致毛利率較前季下滑0.6pt 至 20%， 不過在費
用控管下，OPM 較前季回升 0.4pt 至 9.8%。單季稅後 EPS 3.13 元，
低於原預期的 3.64 元 ， 且明顯低於前季的7.9 元 ， 差異主要係2Q26
認列較多 WAF 金融評價利益。

2.美光支付 5 億美元並非是預付款，而是認購 CB:  
今年 7 月環球晶與美光宣布簽訂 10 年期 LTA，美光將支付環球晶 5
億美元，然而，並非以預付款形式支付，而是環球晶發行上限 5 億
美元的可轉債，由美光擔任唯一應募人。研究員認為，客戶支付預
付款綁定產能的強制力相對較強 (預付款在出貨後會轉營收且不退
還)，然而，CB 存在股本稀釋的問題(推估約 3%上下)，相對較不受
歡迎。

### Page 2

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  2

3.2027 年起開始適用新 LTA 價格，ASP 將改善:  
在價格趨勢方面，公司對於價格調整將採取逐案談判 (Case-by-
case)，依據產品規格、供需狀況、合約到期時間與個別客戶情況進
行協議，非全產品線統一調漲特定百分比。公司認為目前市場環境
日益支持價格回歸合理水準，且已有多筆新 LTA 接近洽談完成，預
計自 2027 年起整體 ASP 將受惠於新 LTA 與產品組合優化而有所改
善。

3.4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26，但受限產能，且價格仍沿用舊合約:  
展望 4Q26，義大利 Novara 廠已正式啟動部分復工，且整體客戶需
求動能依然非常強勁 ， 甚至有客戶願意提前適用新價格以爭取產能 ，
但受限於產能不足 ， 且4Q26 絕大多數價格仍適用舊合約 ， 因此預期
4Q26 營收會優於 3Q26， 但仍無法達成雙位數%成長 。 不過自1Q27 起，
隨著新產能開出以及 ASP 提升，營運動能將轉佳。研究員預估2026
年營收-1.0%YoY，稅後EPS 17.82 元(原估 19.49 元，下修主要是因
為火災)。

4.2027 年營運將明顯好轉，大致維持獲利預估:  
展望 2027 年 ， 根據研調統計 ， 開始營運投片的新Fab 數量將自 2026
年的 13 座大幅增加至 2027 年的 23 座 ， 且成熟製程庫存健康化 ，電
源管理、車用與工業恢復，公司預期 2027 年矽晶圓供需環境將比
2026 年更吃緊。就環球晶而言，自 1Q27 起隨著新產能開出，且開
始適用調漲後的新 LTA 價格，將有助於營收及獲利轉強。此外，隨
著 AI 資料中心發展 ，SiC 市場正步入新成長週期 ，12 吋 SOI 晶圓也
受惠矽光子強勁動能推升需求，密蘇里州廠是目前美國唯一具備12
吋 SOI 製造能力的基地，產能持續爬升，客戶意向及預付款已可涵
蓋擬新增產能的 90%以上。基於以上，環球晶將步入產業上升循環，
預估 2027 年營收+33.8%YoY，稅後 EPS 33.14 元(原估 32.53 元)。

## Document 205: [000270] 기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결기아(000270) 물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결 - 기업

주가 추이

견조한 판매 확대로 매출 견조, 이익은 시장 기대치 하회 예상 
3Q26 동사 매출액과 영업이익은 각각 33.6조원(+17.3% 이하 Y oY)과 2.39조
원(+63.6%) 기록 전망. 3Q 매출액은 친환경차 중심 신차 확대 통해 두 자릿수 
성장세를 기록. 3Q 판매량은 도매 기준 84.3만대(+7.6% 이하 Y oY)로 전년 동
기 대비 성장 폭을 확대했고, 특히 친환경차 판매량 29.8만대(+45.9%)를 기록
하며 이번 분기 매출 성장에 절대적으로 기여.  
다만, 3Q 수익성은 4~5월 상승했던 원자재 투입 영향과 ‘경제형EV 믹스’가 더
욱 확대(13.2% → 14.5%)되면서 단기 수익성에 부정적 작용하며 시장 기대치 
2.5조원을 다소 하회할 전망. 그러나, 기말 환율이 1,356원/$ 수준으로 낮 아 지
면서 전년 동기 수 익 성 악화에 크게 영 향 을 미쳤던 판매보증비 외화환산손익 
영향이 개선(1.6%p 수준) 기대되며, 관세 부담 또한 북미 HEV 확대로 전년 동
기 대비 약 4,500억원(2.0%p) 감소하면서 부정적 수익성 영향 만회 전망.  
유럽은 점유율 확대, 북미는 수익성 개선으로 
올해 북미 HEV와 유럽 BEV 중심 신차 출시 및 물량 확대가 본격화되면
서, ’27년에는 이익 성장 기여도가 확대될 것으로 전망. 미국 조 지 아 텔 루 라 이
드 HEV 신규 생산(2.8만대) 및 HMGMA 스 포 티 지HEV신규 생산(1.0만
대) 등 수 요 가 높은 북미 현 지 에 서 HEV 물량 확대 중으로 4Q에도 텔루라이
드 HEV와 스포티지 HEV의 현지 생산/판매 확대를 비롯하여 셀토스 HEV 
출시로 ’27년 북미 중심 HEV 신차 물량 증가 및 믹스 개선 기대. 유럽은 강한 
BEV 수요가 지속되면서 중국 업체들의 공세 강화 중이나 동사의 물량 확대 또
한 견조. 3Q EV3 2.9만대, EV5 2.2만대 등 유럽 BEV 수요 대응 전략 주효. ’27
년에는 K4/셀토스 및 쏘렌토 등 HEV 신차 중 심 의 친환경차 PT 믹스 다양화
로 유럽 내 물량 확대 흐름 지속 전망 
물량 확대는 증익으로 귀결, 기말 배당 매력도 부각 
’27년 동사 영업이익 추 정 치 는 11.1조원(+13.5%)으로 증익 지속 전망. 최근 
환율 추세 고려 시 내년 수익성에 부 정 적 영향 확대 가능성 존재하나, 올해 확
인된 신차 중심 물량 확대를 통해 부정적 환율 영향을 상쇄할 것 으 로 기대. 
12M EPS 추정치 하향(-2.9%) 및 상 반 기 적 용 한 코스피 프리미엄 제거(-
10%)로 목 표 주 가 를 260,000원으로 하향. 그러나, RA 설립에서의 동사 지분 
참여 및 기말 배당(’26년 DPS 7,000원 추정)에 따른 상 대 적 배당 수익 매력
도 부각 등은 주가 모 멘 텀 에 있어 기대 가능한 요소.

[ Compliance Notice ] (공표일: 2026 년 10월 8 일) 
이 자료는 조사분석 담당자가 객관적 사실에 근거해 작성하였으며, 타인의 부당한 압력이나 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 
반영했습니다. 본인은 이 자료에서 다룬 종목과 관련해 공표일 현재 관련 법규상 알려야 할 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 본인은 이 
자료를 기관투자자 또는 제 3 자에게 사전에 제공한 사실이 없습니다. (김성래) 
저희 회사는 공표일 현재 이 자료에서 다룬 종목의 발행주식을 1% 이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
이 자료는 투자자의 증권투자를 돕기 위해 당사 고객에 한하여 배포되는 자료로서 저작권이 당사에 있으며 불법 복제 및 배포를 금
합니다. 이 자료에 수록된 내용은 당사 리서치센터가 신뢰할 만한 자료나 정보출처로부터 얻은 것이지만, 당사는 그 정확성이나 완
전성을 보장할 수 없습니다. 따라서 이 자료는 어떠한 경우에도 고객의 증권투자 결과와 관련된 법적 책임소재에 대한 증빙으로 사
용될 수 없습니다.

### Page 8

기아 (000270) [한화리서치]

8 
[ 종목 투자등급 ] 
당사는 개별 종목에 대해 향후 1 년간 +15% 이상의 절대수익률이 기대되는 종목에 대해 Buy(매수) 의견을 제시합니다. 또한 절대수
익률 -15~+15%가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Hold(보유) 의견을, -15% 이하가 예상되는 종목에 대해 Sell(매도) 의견을 제시합니다. 밸류
에이션 방법 등 절대수익률 산정은 개별 종목을 커버하는 애널리스트의 추정에 따르며, 목표주가 산정이나 투자의견 변경 주기는 종
목별로 다릅니다.

[ 산업 투자의견 ] 
당사는 산업에 대해 향후 1 년간 해당 업종의 수익률이 과거 수익률에 비해 양호한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Positive(긍정
적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 또한 향후 1 년간 수익률이 과거 수익률과 유사한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Neutral(중립적) 
의견을, 과거 수익률보다 부진한 흐름을 보일 것으로 예상되는 경우에 Negative(부정적) 의견을 제시하고 있습니다. 산업별 수익률 전
망은 해당 산업 내 분석대상 종목들에 대한 담당 애널리스트의 분석과 판단에 따릅니다.

[ 당사 조사분석자료의 투자등급 부여 비중 ] (기준일: 2026 년 9 월 30 일) 
투자등급 매수 중립 매도 합계

## Document 249: [207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458

Source PDF: all report pdf/[207940] 삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더할 모멘텀삼성바이오로직스(207940) 신규 공장이 채운 실적, 수주가 더_21961458.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-10-07
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
미국 록빌·5공장 가세로 3분기 기대치 부합 전망 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 3분기 연결 매출액은 1조 3,637억 원(+8.4% Y oY), 
영업이익은 6,092억 원(-3.8% YoY, 영업이익률 44.7%)으로 시장 기대치에 부합할 
전망이다. 5월 파업에 따른 생산 차질에도 1~4공장(총 60.4만 리터)의 풀가동과 
신규 공장의 매출 기여가 외형 성장을 뒷받침할 것으로 예상한다. 미국 록빌 
공장(6만 리터)은 3분기부터 매출 기여가 본격화되고, 5공장(18만 리터)도 
인증용(PPQ) 배치 생산을 통해 매출이 확대될 전망이다. 2분기에는 미국 공장·5공장 
관련 비용이 선반영됐으나, 3분기에는 매출 증가에 따른 고정비 부담 완화로 
영업이익률이 전분기 대비 0.3%p 상승할 것으로 예상한다. 
항체 중심 CDMO에서 펩타이드로 확장 
삼성바이오로직스의 2026년 신규 수주는 약 1.2조 원으로 전년 수주액의 약 20% 
수준에 그치고 있으나, 연말부터 내년까지 수주 모멘텀은 점차 강화될 것으로 
판단한다. 2025년 하반기 이후 미국의 의약품 관세 불확실성이 완화되며 글로벌 
제약사와의 논의가 늘어나고 있어, 신규 수주 계약으로 이어질 것으로 기대된다. 
11월 유상증자 납입이 마무리되면 6공장(18만 리터) 착공과 스위스 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 본격화되며 중장기 성장 기대감도 높아질 전망이다. 특히 폴리펩타이드 
인수가 연내 완료되면 2027년부터 연결 실적에 반영되는 동시에, 항체 의약품 
중심의 CDMO 사업을 GLP-1 등 펩타이드로 확장하는 계기가 될 전망이다. 
목표주가 200만 원으로 하향, 수주 회복이 관건 
삼성바이오로직스의 목표주가를 209만 원에서 200만 원으로 하향한다. 유상증자 
신주 발행에 따른 주당가치 4.7% 희석 효과를 반영했다. 폴리펩타이드 인수 효과는 
거래완료 이후 실적 추정치에 반영할 예정이다. 노조 파업 불확실성이 주가에 
부담으로 작용할 수 있어 신규 수주 모멘텀의 회복이 필요한 시점이다. 
목표주가 (하향) 2,000,000원

동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

종목명 담당자 담당자(배우자) 보유여부 1%이상 보유여부 유가증권 발행관련 계열사 관계여부 공개매수 사무취급 IPO 회사채 지급보증 중대한 이해관계 M&A 관련 수량 취득가 취득일

## Document 277: AI抛售潮血洗亚洲富豪榜，孙正义、三星家族身家遭重创！

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI抛售潮血洗亚洲富豪榜，孙正义、三星家族身家遭重创！.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

据彭博亿万富翁指数，亚洲 22 位顶级富豪⼀⽇财富缩⽔逾 180 亿美元（约合⼈⺠币 1200 亿
元），软银创始⼈孙正义损失 39 亿美元，三星李在镕家族亦遭重创，合计财富缩⽔约 25 亿美
元。
摘要：
全球科技股抛售潮向亚洲蔓延，重创 AI 产业链核⼼企业，令亚洲顶级富豪财富在⼀⽇之内⼤幅缩
⽔。
据彭博亿万富翁指数，截⾄⾹港时间周五上午 10 时，来⾃⽇本、韩国等国家和地区、跻⾝全球
500 ⼤富豪榜的 22 位亿万富翁合计财富蒸发逾 210 亿美元，此后跌幅有所收窄，最终缩⽔逾 180
亿美元（约合⼈⺠币 1200 亿元）。
其中，软银集团创始⼈孙正义（ Masayoshi Son ）损失最为惨重，个⼈财富单⽇缩⽔ 39 亿美
元，降⾄约 290 亿美元。此轮抛售潮紧随纳斯达克 100 指数创纪录的 2.3 万亿美元市值蒸发之后，
并进⼀步加剧了英伟达 CEO ⻩仁勋跌出全球⼗⼤富豪的财富逆转态势。
市场动荡的导⽕索在于，外界预期⺠主党同时掌控众议院与参议院后，将推动放缓数据中⼼建

设 —— 这⼀ AI 基础设施的核⼼环节 —— 从⽽拖累亚洲科技股全线⾛低。与此同时，据知情⼈⼠透
露，此轮波动还令台湾⾄⾹港⼀带券商的富裕客⼾⾯临杠杆仓位的追加保证⾦压⼒。
孙正义领衔， AI 产业链富豪⾸当其冲
此次财富缩⽔最集中于 AI 产业链核⼼企业的相关股东。
单⽇损失逾 10 亿美元的富豪包括：电⼦传感器制造商基恩⼠（ Keyence Corp. ）的滝崎武光，以
及新加坡⾸富、半导体封测企业⽇⽉光投控（ ASE Technology Holding Co. ）的张虔⽣ (Jason
Chang) 。
孙正义以 39 亿美元（约合⼈⺠币 260 亿元）的单⽇损失位居榜⾸，其财富降⾄约 290 亿美元。软
银集团旗下持有⼤量科技及 AI 相关资产，使其财富⾛势与全球科技股⾏情⾼度绑定。
三星家族集体受损，韩国科技股承压
韩国⽅⾯，三星电⼦⼤股东李在镕及其⺟亲、姐妹 —— 亚洲最富有的家族之⼀ —— 合计财富缩
⽔约 25 亿美元，成为此轮抛售潮中损失最为集中的家族群体之⼀。
三星电⼦作为全球半导体产业的核⼼企业，其股价⾛势直接牵动李⽒家族的财富规模，此次跌

势亦折射出市场对 AI 芯⽚需求前景的重新审视。
对冲基⾦同步受压，杠杆资⾦⾯临追保
此轮市场动荡的冲击范围不仅限于亚洲富豪群体。
据彭博报道， Tiger Global Management 及 Whale Rock Capital Management 等对冲基⾦上⽉已
因 AI 投资前景的不确定性⽽遭受冲击。

China: Trade to remain strong as inﬂation
edges lower
China will release October inﬂation and trade data. We expect CPI

inﬂation to edge down to 0.8% YoY, from 0.9% in September,
suggesting that consumer price pressures remain subdued amid
continued weakness in domestic demand. By contrast, PPI inﬂation
is expected to rise further to 5.3% YoY, from 5.0%, driven mainly by
elevated energy and commodity prices.
Export and import growth are expected to accelerate to 40.7% YoY

technology-related and manufactured goods and increased

commodity imports. The trade surplus is expected to narrow
slightly to $131.2bn, from $133.5bn in September, as import
growth accelerates.
Taiwan: Technology demand supports strong
trade
Taiwan releases October trade data on Monday. We expect export
growth to moderate to 51.3% YoY, from 60.9% in September. Even
so, growth should remain exceptionally strong, supported by
demand for semiconductors and other AI-related products. Imports
are expected to accelerate to 69.2% YoY, from 51.7%, reﬂecting

strong demand for semiconductor equipment, components and
advanced materials. Despite faster import growth, the trade surplus
is forecast to widen to $25.6bn, from $23.6bn in September,
highlighting the continued strength of Taiwan’s technology export
cycle.

## Document 336: First Impressions_ NZ labour market surveys, September quarter 2026 (2026-11-07)

Source PDF: all report pdf/First Impressions_ NZ labour market surveys, September quarter 2026 (2026-11-07).pdf
Report source: Westpac IQ
Date: 2026-11-07
Pages: 10
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

#labour #newzealand
First Impressions: NZ labour market surveys, September quarter
2026
By Michael Gordon
Senior Economist, Westpac NZ
09:01 November 04 2026 Share
The unemployment rate rose to 5.7% in the September quarter, against expectations for a
small decline. Spare capacity in the labour market is keeping wage pressures subdued.
Unemployment rate: 5.7% (prev: 5.6%, Westpac: 5.5%, RBNZ: 5.5%, mkt: 5.5%)
Employment change: -0.1% (prev: +0.3%, Westpac: +0.5%, RBNZ: +0.3%, mkt: +0.4%)

### Page 2

Participation rate: 70.4% (prev: 70.6%, Westpac: 70.8%, RBNZ: 70.6%, mkt: 70.7%) 
Labour costs (private sector): +0.5% (prev: +0.7%, Westpac: +0.5%, RBNZ: +0.4%, mkt: +0.5%)

The September quarter surveys pointed to ongoing softness in New Zealand’s labour market. The
unemployment rate rose from 5.6% to 5.7%, continuing an almost five-year run of increases.
Employment was softer than expected, and wage growth remains subdued despite increasing cost-of-
living pressures. 
The Household Labour Force Survey (HLFS) showed a surprise 0.1% fall in employment in the
September quarter, after a downwardly revised 0.3% increase in the June quarter. That went against
the Monthly Employment Indicator (MEI) measure of filled jobs, which had suggested some upward
momentum emerging in the jobs market (and notably, the HLFS measure had outpaced the MEI for the
previous nine quarters). It was also softer than suggested by the business-focused Quarterly
Employment Survey (QES), which recorded a 0.2% rise in full-time equivalent employees, a 0.1% rise
in filled jobs and a 0.6% rise in hours paid. 
The fall in HLFS employment was accompanied by a fall in labour force participation rate, from 70.6%
to 70.4%. These co-movements are effectively a reversal of the surprisingly large increases that we
saw in the June quarter, which we noted at the time were indicative of sampling error in the survey. 
This is why we generally recommend focusing on the unemployment rate, as it’s less vulnerable to
sampling error. And in that respect, the message on the jobs market remains weak: to the extent that
employment may be rising, it’s still not fast enough to absorb the growth in the population (which is
itself a fairly low bar, as net migration remains at a relatively low ebb). 
The HLFS also recorded a sharp 1.6% fall in hours worked (although usual hours worked were down
just 0.4%). This seems to have been influenced by Matariki – a floating holiday that was only
introduced a few years ago, so will not be picked up by the seasonal adjustment. This may have also
been responsible for a marked shift from full-time to part-time employment, as some people may have
been pushed into the part-time category simply by virtue of only working four days during the survey
week. 
The Labour Cost Index (LCI) rose by 0.5% in the September quarter, in line with our expectations
overall but a bit weaker in the details – private sector wages were up by 0.5%, while there was a
surprisingly large 0.7% increase in public sector wages. There was a notable jump in the number of
employers citing cost-of-living adjustments as a reason for increasing pay rates, although the size of
those increases varied a great deal, with many substantially less than the headline inflation rate. 
Overall, we think the details are soft from the RBNZ’s perspective – an important consideration ahead
of the 9 December Monetary Policy Statement, as there are few other major data releases between
now and then. The unemployment rate is a more reliable signal than the employment measures, and
the ongoing rise in unemployment points to more slack in the labour market than the RBNZ was hoping
to see in an economy that has been growing for over a year now. The doves on the policy committee
will be encouraged in their view that there is limited scope for headline inflation shocks to pass through
into second-round effects. 
Michael Gordon, Senior Economist
P: +64 21 749 506

## Document 375: More signs of life in Hungary’s economy

Source PDF: all report pdf/More signs of life in Hungary’s economy.pdf
Report source: ING Think
Date: 2026-11-06
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Both industry and retail sales expanded in September. Positive
surprises appeared in both sets of data. As Hungary’s growth is
expected to be supported by internal factors, the external
inﬂuence remains the only question mark in the recovery story

Peter Virovacz and Zoltán Homolya
Industry and retail sales surprised to the upside in September. We think both
sectors will contribute positively to economic growth as we head into year-end
The Hungarian Central Statistical Oﬃce (HCSO) has released
September data on retail sales and industrial production. As we
move closer to the end of the year, it is becoming increasingly clear
that both sectors are set to contribute positively to economic
growth, in line with our expectations. Industrial production
maintained its recent growth momentum, reaching a level not seen
in two and a half years. Retail sales also rebounded after August’s
negative surprise, despite elevated fuel prices. Strong consumer
conﬁdence and rising real disposable incomes are expected to keep
consumption on an upward trend in the coming months.

New capacities are pulling up Hungarian

Hungarian industrial performance delivered a signiﬁcant positive
surprise in September. Based on the pattern observed in recent
months, a monthly decline had been expected. Instead, industrial
production volumes increased by 0.9% month-on-month in the
ninth month of the year. Owing to last year’s low base, the
working-day-adjusted year-on-year index jumped to 8.2%. The

positive momentum continued for a third consecutive month,
suggesting that the previous sawtooth pattern may ﬁnally have
been broken. As a result, the ﬁxed-base index relative to the 2021
average moved into positive territory in September for the ﬁrst time
in more than two years, in line with our earlier calls. After more
than three years of decline and recession, this marks an important
milestone in the industrial recovery.

As this is a preliminary data release, HCSO has not yet disclosed
many details. Based on its brief commentary, the now-familiar
pattern of concentrated growth remains in place. With new
automotive production capacities coming online, vehicle
manufacturing performed exceptionally well, as did the
manufacture of computers, electronic and optical products,
supported by rising electronics orders linked to the AI boom. It is
also a positive development that electrical equipment

manufacturing, which carries signiﬁcant weight, has joined the
group of positive contributors. By contrast, the HCSO recorded a
decline in the manufacture of food products, beverages and
tobacco products, which may be related to the negative
performance of agriculture this year.
Looking ahead, the soft indicators also point to the continuation of
the improving trend. The business conﬁdence index remains on an
upward trajectory, with October data showing a further increase

despite higher energy prices. This reinforces the narrative that,
notwithstanding geopolitical risks, the years of stagnation may
now be ﬁrmly behind us. Developments in order books also suggest
that the ramp-up of new manufacturing capacities will continue to
provide meaningful momentum for industry, although growth may
remain concentrated in a limited number of segments.

Turning to the risks: On the upside, the better-than-expected
resilience of the European economy, particularly the recovery in

than currently anticipated. On the downside, oil prices around $100

In the ﬁrst nine months of the year, industrial output has already

around 3-4% may need an upward revision. The positive surprise
could also lay the foundations for stable growth in the coming
years, as the industrial turnaround no longer seems to be in
question. The next major test will be whether growth can spread
from a few isolated pockets to the sector as a whole.

After the unpleasant surprise in August, Hungarian retail sales
moved higher in September. Sales volumes rose by 1.1% compared

expected following the weak performance of the previous month,

Looking at the longer-term trend, retail sales volumes in the ninth
month of 2026 were 7.7% above the monthly average recorded in
2021. This marks the strongest performance by Hungarian retail
since the summer of 2022 and, despite monthly ﬂuctuations, the
underlying trend remains upward.
Turning to the details of September’s outstanding performance,
perhaps the biggest surprise was the marked increase in fuel sales.
Sales rose by almost 2% on a monthly basis, which is certainly

surprising given the continued sharp increase in fuel prices.
Sales at grocery stores contracted on a monthly basis, albeit only
marginally. By contrast, sales volumes at non-food retailers
increased signiﬁcantly. Pharmaceutical sales jumped sharply, while
computer shops, furniture and electrical goods stores, and
manufactured goods in non-specialised shops also had a strong
month. While in-store sales posted meaningful growth across
almost every segment, mail-order and online retail performed more

modestly this time. For now, it is diﬃcult to identify a clear
fundamental explanation for the broad-based strengthening in
September. It may therefore partly reﬂect a correction following
August’s weakness, potentially combined with some purchases
that had been postponed during the unusually hot summer
months.

Looking ahead, the foundations for continued growth in retail sales,
and consequently in household consumption, remain favourable.

Consumer conﬁdence remains high by historical standards despite
a recent correction, while relatively moderate inﬂation and strong
nominal wage growth continue to support households’ purchasing
power.
We therefore expect retail sales to remain on an upward trend over
the remainder of the year, albeit with considerable month-to-
month volatility. For 2026 as a whole, we continue to expect growth
of around 4–5%. Moreover, next year’s planned economic policy

measures are speciﬁcally aimed at supporting lower-income
households, which typically have a higher propensity to consume.
This could provide an additional boost to retail activity and
household consumption.

## Document 419: Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01)

Source PDF: all report pdf/Threats to European diesel imports_ risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased risk of price rises (2026-10-01).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-10-01
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Paris, 1st October 2026

EcoFlash

Threats to European diesel imports: risk of shortages ruled out for the time being, but increased 
risk of price rises

In Europe, the possibility of a US ban on diesel exports has heightened concerns about price trends for this widely used 
fuel1, which has already risen significantly since the outbreak of the conflict in Iran. It has become a major political issue 
on both sides of the Atlantic in the run-up to decisive elections.

A particularly tight diesel market due to a shortage of buffer stocks 
Following the outbreak of the conflict, diesel prices did indeed see some of the sharpest rises on the oil market due to particularly 
tight market conditions. Since the end of February, whilst the price of a barrel of crude has risen by 46%, diesel prices have soared 
by 100% in Europe and by 76% in the United States.

The factors alleviating pressure on the refined products market, and more specifically the diesel market, were more limited than in 
the case of crude oil. Shipments passing through the Strait of Hormuz (around 17% of maritime diesel trade prior to the conf lict) 
have fallen by around 75%. Added to this is the halt in Russian exports due to war damage, which is depriving the diesel market of 
around 12% of its maritime exports. Furthermore, unlike with crude oil, China has not acted as a stabilising  force in the diesel 
market. On the contrary, the energy crisis has led to a halt in Chinese exports of refined products (including diesel) to the  rest of 
Asia from April onwards. The resumption of Chinese exports over the last two months remains fragile, given, in particular, that 
diesel stocks are at their lowest level in 15 months.

The United States has eased these tensions in the diesel market by increasing its exports of refined products (up 25% on average 
in the first half of 2026 compared with the average over the last five years). This rise in exports has required high refinery utilisation 
rate (close to maximum capacity) and, to a lesser extent, a reduction in stocks (-11% compared with the average over the last five 
years as at mid -September for middle distillates 2). All these factors have contributed to tightening the US domestic market and 
pushed retail diesel prices to record levels. Road diesel prices (US average) have risen by 68% since the end of February and by 
74% year-on-year.

A structurally high level of vulnerability in Europe… 
Against this market backdrop, characterised by mounting tensions and very limited room for manoeuvre , Europe would be 
particularly hard hit by a reduction in – or even a complete halt to – US exports. According to Eurostat, imports accounted for 46% 
of European demand for diesel in the first half of 2026, with 40% of these imports coming from the United States. Europe, which

1 Diesel accounts for 17% of the primary energy mix in the EU and 7.3% in the United States. 
2 A category of refined petroleum products comprising mainly diesel and aviation fuel.

### Page 2

has lost a great deal of production capacity in recent years, is operating its refineries at near -maximum capacity. Its room for 
manoeuvre is therefore limited.

… But the risk of a shortage has been ruled out in the short term 
The risk of a shortage, however, appears limited in the short term. The situation regarding European stocks is not comfortable, but 
it is not alarming. Commercial diesel stocks in the ARA region 3 are currently at historically low levels (around 12 million barrels), 
23% below their level at the end of September 2025. As for emergency stocks (EU Emergency Stocks), these are, according to the 
European Commission, at a sufficient level. Eurostat estim ates this figure at 270 million barrels in June 2026 (stable from a year 
earlier), representing approximately 60 days’ worth of EU consumption. Europe imports around 0.9 mb/d of diesel from the United 
States, and emergency stocks would therefore cover around 60 days’ consumption at 4.5 mb/d.

The rise in prices could be significant 
Whatever the outcome of the conflict, diesel prices are likely to continue to rise in the short term. According to our base -case 
scenario, crude oil prices are expected to remain very high in Q4 2026 due to ongoing tensions in the Persian Gulf, whilst a return 
to normal levels of diesel exports from Russia and the Middle East appears unlikely by that time.

Among the factors likely to cause future tensions, the start of the maintenance season in Europe could affect production leve ls, 
whilst demand for diesel in the United States is typically high in the autumn (agricultural harvests and pre -winter storage). I n a 
scenario where tensions remain low, the diesel refining margin (crack spread) could rise by 5% in Q4 compared with its level at the 
end of September 2026, before gradually falling back during 2027. An intermediate scenario (a 10% rise in the crack spread in Q4 
2026) would result in moderate constraints on US diesel exports and a drawdown of European stocks. Finally, an adverse scenario 
(a 35% rise in the crack spread in Q4 2026) would imply severe restrictions on US diesel exports and a delayed European response.

What impact will this have on inflation in the euro area? 
According to our estimates, the rise in diesel prices would exacerbate the expected peak in inflation in the euro area without altering 
its timing. In our baseline scenario for September 2026 (see our recently published EcoPerspective), headline inflation would peak 
at 4.1% y/y in January 2027 before gradually falling back towards 2% by the end of the year. The two least adverse scenarios 
would add only a limited amount to the peak: +0.1 pp in the first scenario (+5% for the crack spread in Q4 compared with the end 
of Q3) and +0.2 pp in the intermediate scenario (+10%), with inflation reaching 4.2% and 4.3% respectively in January 2027. 
However, there is an upside risk in the second scenario linked to the uncertain extent of the spillov er effects. In both cases, the 
main impact would be felt through fuel prices (which quickly reflect rising market prices) and would fade over the course of 2027. 
In the case of the adverse scenario (+35%), the additional inflation would be close to 0.6 pp by early 2027, pushing inflation above 
4.6%. A shock of this magnitude would, in fact, reduce businesses’ ability to absorb rising costs, which would lead to price increases 
across a broader range of goods (food, industrial goods). Subsequently, the effect would only fade gradually.

Pascal Devaux 
Economist, Middle East, Western Balkans – Energy 
pascal.devaux@bnpparibas.com

Guillaume Derrien 
Economist, Europe, Eurozone, International Trade 
guillaume.a.derrien@bnpparibas.com

3 Amsterdam-Rotterdam-Antwerp, Europe’s main hub for the storage of raw materials.

## Document 429: United States _ A shock-resistant expansion (2026-09-24)

Source PDF: all report pdf/United States _ A shock-resistant expansion (2026-09-24).pdf
Report source: BNP Paribas
Date: 2026-09-24
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

6
economic-research.bnpparibas.comEcoPerspectives / 3rd Quarter 2026 / September 2026
The bank
for a changing
world
A SHOCK-RESISTANT EXPANSION
US growth remains resilient despite successive shocks. According to our forecasts, it is expected to reach 2.2% in 2026 and
2.3% in 2027. Its main driver is still investment in artificial intelligence (AI), which has remained entirely unaffected by the
energy crisis. The labour market has seen a marked improvement in job creation and a fall in the unemployment rate. Core

inflation remains stubbornly high and above the Federal Reserve (Fed)’s target. Against this backdrop, the Fed has begun a
hiking cycle in September, which, according to our scenario, would bring the Fed Funds target to 4.50% (upper limit) by Q1
2027. At the same time, long-term rates are under upward pressure.
1  This includes: energy and communications; data centres (facilities); IT equipment and peripherals; communication equipment (equipment); software; and research and develop-

ment (intellectual-property products).
THE ENERGY SHOCK IS NOT HOLDING BACK GROWTH
Driven by AI-related investment, US growth is holding up despite ri -
sing energy inflation. We forecast GDP growth at 2.2% in 2026 and
2.3% in 2027 on an annual average basis. The AI-related investment
cycle1 (+14.8% y/y in H1 2026) is expected to continue, accompanied by
potential productivity gains. However, other private-investment seg -
ments (including residential property) remain sluggish. In addition, the

rising cost of capital could accentuate its ‘K-shaped’ profile. Household
consumption is expected, on the whole, to benefit from the rebound in
job creation and to remain above 2% y/y through to the end of 2027.
In H1 2026, it held up against resurging inflation due to an average
year-on-year increase of 11.5% in tax refunds and a savings rate stan-
ding at its lowest level since 2022 (2.8% in Q2).
RECORD CAPITAL-RAISING DRIVEN BY AI
This year, AI-related investments are driving massive capital-raising

by companies through loans, bond issues or share placements. This
trend is set to continue given the seemingly colossal scale of the fun -
ding requirements, even as risk premia in the bond market begin to
widen. According to their statements, the five tech giants could invest
nearly USD 900 bn this year and over USD 1,000 bn next year—more
than double the figure for 2025. Spurred on by regulatory easing, the
major US banks have announced that they will mobilise significant re-

sources to support projects considered strategic for the United States
(AI, defence, energy and infrastructure). While funding for the AI boom
appears unaffected by rising long-term interest rates, these latter are
likely to continue to weigh on household demand for mortgages. Since
the start of the conflict in the Middle East, 30-year mortgage rates
have risen by 78 bp (to 6.76% at the start of September). Conversely,
consumer lending remained buoyant in H1 2026 and is supporting

household spending.
TWO-SPEED GROWTH IN INDUSTRY
Industrial production rebounded slightly in 2025 (+1.1%), driven by AI
and aerospace. We forecast an acceleration to +1.5% in 2026 and +2%
in 2027. Investment in AI is bolstering the electronics sector, while the
(civil and military) aerospace sector is rebounding as order backlogs
are cleared and defence requirements rise. Conversely, sectors ex -
posed to rising tariffs (in particular, the steel, aluminium and auto -

motive sectors) are still on the back burner, contrary to the objective
of these measures. This positive momentum in industrial production,
while concentrated, is expected to continue, bolstered by the recove -
ry in business sentiment. The ISM Manufacturing Index has been in
expansionary territory since early 2026, despite ongoing pressures on
prices and supply chains.
UNITED STATES
Growth and inflation

world
LABOUR MARKET: A LOW UNEMPLOYMENT RATE ON A DOWNWARD
TREND
Job creation (non-farm payrolls) has rebounded, averaging +80,000 per
month since the start of the year, compared with +10,000 in 2025. This
growth is no longer limited to the healthcare sector. Excluding health-
care, job creation is up by an average of +38,000 in 2026, compared

with -47,000 in 2025. Manufacturing and construction, two sectors
driven by AI, are contributing to this rebound. At the same time, the
labour force has not grown since January 2025 due to the decline in the
foreign-born workforce (down 4.2% cumulatively over the period). Ex -
cluding the impact of COVID-19, the labour force participation rate is at
its lowest level since the 1970s, standing at around 61.5%. The rebound
in labour demand and persistent pressure on the labour supply have

brought the unemployment rate down to 4.1% in August 2026 (a 0.3 pp
fall since the start of the year). The jobless rate is expected to fall to
3.9% at the end of 2027 (4.2% corresponds to full employment, accor -
ding to the CBO) thanks to a job creation rate of around 40,000–60,000
per month.
INFLATION: STRUCTURALLY ABOVE TARGET
The rise in oil prices has triggered a rebound in CPI inflation. The
contribution from the energy component (+1.2 pp) largely accounts for

the rise in inflation since February. A slowdown was seen (from +4.2%
to +3.4% y/y) between the May peak and August. This is expected to
continue due to base effects on energy prices. We anticipate the trough
to be +1.8% y/y in Q2 2027 before a stabilisation at around +2.3% y/y
later in the year.
Meanwhile, core inflation is expected to remain above the Fed’s 2%
target, and above +2.5% y/y in 2027. This highlights the structural
nature of inflation, beyond short-term fluctuations linked to energy.

Non-housing services are expected to show significant inertia, with in-
flation hovering at around +3% y/y by the end of 2027. The potential for
disinflation also appears limited for housing-related services, as the
rise in rents shows no signs of abating. Finally, inflation on non-energy
goods is slowing as the effects of tariffs fade. However, the AI cycle is
exerting upward pressure through the general rise in the cost of elec -
tronic goods.
FED: GREEN LIGHT FOR RATE HIKES

The FOMC has embarked on a cycle of rate increases. The Fed Funds
target was raised to 4.0% (+25 bp) at the September meeting, and we
anticipate two further rises that would bring the policy rate to 4.5% in
January 2027. These three rises (made possible by reassuring develop-
ments in employment and economic activity) are intended to reverse
the three cuts in 2025 adopted to counter the ‘downside risks’ to the
labour market at that time. As a result, the committee can recalibrate

its policy towards ‘price stability’ and, through its interest rate deci -
sions, reflect the priority given to this objective in its communications.
The risks point towards further monetary tightening: in our baseline
scenario, core inflation would remain sticky and the fall in unemploy -
ment would give the FOMC room for manoeuvre to raise rates further.
PUBLIC FINANCES: UNDERLYING DETERIORATION AND PRESSURE ON
LONG- TERM RATES
According to our estimates, the federal deficit is set to widen by 0.7 pp

to -6.6% of GDP in 2026 (by 0.4 pp to -3.1% for the primary deficit),
weighed down by falling tariff revenue (refunds of tariffs already col -
lected and lower-than-expected receipts). In 2027, the end of this one-
off effect is expected to lead to a marginal improvement to -6.3% (to
-2.8% for the primary deficit). The growing burden of interest payments
(3.5% of GDP in 2026 and 3.6% in 2027, following 3.2% in 2025) and
the conflict in Iran (affecting the military budget) are weighing on the

budget balance and federal-debt dynamics. Federal debt is still on an
upward trend, with no particular change in direction, exceeding 100% of
GDP (101.9%, up 3.3 pp, in 2026, and 103.5% in 2027). It is expected to
reach 110% by the end of the decade against a backdrop of budget de-
ficits of close to 6% of GDP , assuming fiscal policy remains unchanged.
Furthermore, the mid-term elections (which see the entire House of
Representatives and one-third of the Senate up for re-election) could

result in a divided Congress or in both chambers changing hands to
the Democratic Party. Should the former occur, this would increase the
risk of a legislative deadlock, while the presidential veto would limit
the Democrats’ ability to influence fiscal policy in the latter scenario.
Against this backdrop, pressure on the bond market is likely to continue
in the coming months. Treasury issuance is rising, fuelled by increa -
sing spending requirements (in areas such as AI, defence and supply

chains). At the same time, shifts in investor demographics are increa-
sing demand volatility, and issuances by companies in the artificial
intelligence sector are intensifying competition for capital. We antici -
pate a 10-year yield of 4.95% in Q1 2027, before a slight easing brought
about by Fed rate rises, which would lend credibility to the price-sta -
bility objective. However, according to our forecast, 10-year yield is
expected to remain close to a 20-year high, at 4.80% in Q4 2027.

THE US RELIES ON IMPORTS FOR ITS GROWTH
The US trade deficit has recently started to rise again. According to
our forecasts, it is expected to account for the same proportion of GDP
in 2026 as it did in 2024 (4.4%). This trend is driven by imports of
AI-related capital goods, which are set to rise from 1.2% to 2.1% of GDP
between 2022 and 2026. Investment in AI is, in fact, import-intensive
(Chart 2), which dampens its positive impact on growth and highlights

the US’s dependence on foreign supplies in a cycle that it is neverthe-
less spearheading.
The additional tariffs have failed to reverse the trend in the trade de -
ficit. Furthermore, the IEEPA tariffs struck down by the Supreme Court
were replaced, in July 2026, by ‘Section 301’ tariffs (forced labour) ran-
ging from 10% to 12.5%. These tariffs target 60 countries and have
been introduced on top of existing sector-specific tariffs (steel, alumi -

nium and automotive). Following these adjustments, the statutory rate
(measured against the 2024 import structure) remained slightly above
10%. The main risks relate to potential new sector-specific tariffs and
the renegotiation of the USMCA.
Article completed on 17 September 2026
Anis Bensaidani
anis.bensaidani@bnpparibas.com
Céline Choulet
celine.choulet@bnpparibas.com

## Document 448: What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it_ (2026-10-11)

Source PDF: all report pdf/What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it_ (2026-10-11).pdf
Report source: CFR (Follow the Money)
Date: 2026-10-11
Pages: 17
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

1
EXPERT TAKE FOLLOW THE MONEY
What’s the Renminbi Got to Do With it?
A closer look at the impact of China’s currency on China’s export growth.
Sean Yong/Reuters
UPDATED
October 11, 2026 12:00 a.m.
Articles Follow the Money Share
Log In
Regions Topics Research Experts Events The Future of American Strategy

### Page 2

2
Brad W. Setser CFR EXPERT
Whitney Shepardson Senior Fellow
There is an argument—made in the recent Geneva
Report and by various former IMF officials—that
China’s current export surge is the simple
consequence of the housing downturn and the
resulting weakness in Chinese internal demand.*
The weakness in the RMB in this story is entirely
incidental to the increase in exports, which is, more or
less, a mechanical response to weak internal demand.

Cannot sell excavators at home? Export them.
Built more modern car capacity than your internal
market supports? Export that too…
The fall in the nominal RMB over the course of 2022
and the first half of 2023 in nominal and read terms
was simply a function of domestic macroeconomic
weakness, and a growing rate differential. It wasn’t an
active policy choice by China’s central bank.

### Page 3

3
Now, some might note that China’s currency doesn’t
always move with rate differentials. The RMB didn’t
consistently get pulled up when Chinese rates were
higher than U.S. rates (2002-2020 generally
speaking), and while it did fall when rate differentials
moved against the RMB in 2022 and 2023, it then
stabilized and has even appreciated modestly since
the middle of last year. That appreciation hasn’t been
big enough to correct the deep undervaluation of the

RMB, but it came at a time when the PBOC policy
rate (the reverse repo rate) was well below the U.S.
policy rate.
China doesn’t have an open financial account. As a
result, equilibrium in the foreign exchange market is
thus driven far more by exporter conversion and
current account flows than by rate differentials and
financial flows.** That, together with China’s ongoing
management of the exchange rate and a trade surplus
that can generate significant inflows, means that the

### Page 4

4
value of the RMB is much more of a policy variable
under the independent control of the Chinese
authorities than a simple byproduct of monetary and
fiscal policies.
The modern IMF is reluctant to accept this
conclusion. Its surveillance has generally ignored
China’s currency management (even if that
management is relatively obvious; see the data on FX
settlement) and its recent comprehensive assessment
of China’s economy completely ignored the balance of

payments and exchange rate management.
But it is pretty obvious to the market, even if the
PBOC, rather absurdly, insists that “market forces
play a decisive role in determining the RMB
exchange rate.”
I doubt the PBOC can win this argument. I agree
with Mark Sobel:
“The renminbi is still heavily managed and the
authorities have acted to slow appreciation. The paper
states that the PBoC phased out ‘regular’ foreign
exchange intervention in 2017. But it has many

means at its disposal to achieve the same effect
through extensive capital controls, administrative
guidance, signalling at the fix and deploying state-
owned commercial banks. These means are heavily
used. Thus, the word ‘regular’ must be taken with a
pinch of salt. The comparison to Japan, which

### Page 5

5
intervenes occasionally, is spurious – the yen largely
floats freely in integrated global markets. The same
cannot be said for the renminbi.”
In reality, the PBOC’s daily fix now plays the decisive
role in the currency’s path, and the market is often a
bet on how the PBOC’s management of the yuan will
evolve.
That is clear from the lack of volatility in the yuan’s
rate against the dollar. The yuan’s move down against
the dollar in 2022 was a controlled 10 percent move,

and the appreciation over the last 16 months has been
even more controlled crawl.
And the market is clearly massaged with substantial
help from the state banks. In fact that the pattern of
changes in the monthly FX settlement data looks
exactly like the pattern of intervention of a central
bank managing its currency around a tight band (the

### Page 6

6
state banks in practice buy FX to keep the CNY from
testing the strong side of the band, so there is an
asymmetry in how the CNY is managed)
None of this would matter much if China’s economy
was small. But that obviously isn’t the case. Nor would
this matter much if China’s exports didn’t respond to
changes in the RMB’s real (and nominal) value. But
that is also not the case.
There is abundant evidence that China’s exports do
respond to moves in the currency. Periods of RMB

weakness have consistently been associated with
periods of especially rapid Chinese export growth.

### Page 7

7
Chancellor Merz and President Macron got this
right, even if macroeconomic issues haven’t yet
figured prominently in the EU’s dialogue with
China.*** As they note in their open letter to the
European Commission:
“An appreciation of undervalued currencies towards a value
more in line with fundamentals would help support the
rebalancing of trade accounts with the EU in the short and
medium term … European policy discussions focused on
forging a common understanding of these exchange rate issues

and their interaction with global imbalances would be useful.”
The best evidence that exports responded to changes
in the currency, and not simply to changes in the path
of China’s internal demand, is the acceleration in
China’s export growth after the RMB’s depreciation
over the course of 2022.
This is the killer chart.

### Page 8

8
Now, you might ask: what of the acceleration in
export growth in 2020 and 2021? That was pretty
clearly tied to the global increase in goods demand
that came with the pandemic and work from home
requirements. It was expected to fade when the world
economy normalized. But it didn’t. By 2023 Chinese
export growth again topped global trade growth.
The general expectation that the rise in China’s goods
surplus during Covid would prove to be “transitory”

### Page 9

9
heavily influenced the IMF’s work at the time: see the
IMF’s external sector reports of 2023 and 2024.
I would argue that neglecting the exchange rate was
the key reason the IMF’s kept forecasting, incorrectly,
that China’s current account surplus would fall (See
this blog with Shahin Vallee). The IMF certainly
didn’t warn that China’s property downturn and the
RMB’s real depreciation risked a second China shock
in its 2022, 2023, or 2024 surveillance reports.

Concretely, the weaker RMB appears to have
contributed to a very large outperformance of China’s
exports relative to global trade. See the following
chart
That incidentally also happened after the RMB
followed the dollar down in 2003.
In both periods, there were significant structural
shifts. The WTO accession in 2002, and the success

### Page 10

10
of Chinese industrial policies in the “green” industrial
sectors and autos in 2022. In both cses exports were
turbocharged when structural shifts that should have
resulted in a stronger currency were instead amplified
by currency weakness.
No matter. Paul Krugman has noted that a country
that is converging technologically with its trade
partners should see a trend appreciation in its
currency. China got the technological convergence,
but not the real appreciation—at least not until this

year. And the move since the middle of 2025 has
corrected the depreciation of the RMB that occurred
when the RMB followed the dollar down on
liberation day, but not the structural overvaluation.
Now, there is an argument that exchange rate
adjustment is too hot an issue to make it a priority.
The PBOC isn’t interested it seems.
And some believe that in the interest of comity and
good relations with China, the IMF should mirror
the PBOC’s views. After all a stronger RMB would

slow China’s exports and thus slow China’s economy
at a time when China needs export growth to escape
deflation and generate employment. Or so the
argument goes. Concerns about “external balance“
and the negative global spillovers from China’s rising
goods surplus should be ignored and the Fund’s sole
focus should be on policies that lead to internal

### Page 11

11
balance. That provides one rationale for the Fund’s
ongoing decision to essentially ignore the contentious
issue of the PBOC’s ongoing exchange rate
management.
That argument, however, assumes that China’s
economy is small and thus China’s external surplus
can safely be ignored because it isn’t a drag on the
growth of the IMF’s other members.
That clearly isn’t true.
And it isn’t clear that China is more interested in
having a negotiation over its level of basic pension

benefits or the size of its fiscal deficit (where the
IMF continues to make timid recommendations that
would not move the needle—and certainly wouldn’t
meaningfully reduce China’s external surplus) than a
discussion over its currency practices.****
Back in the world of fixed but adjustable exchange
rates, there was an expectation (not always met to be
sure) that surplus countries would allow their
currencies to strengthen to bring their economies

back into external balance, and that other policy tools
would be used to ensure internal balance.
I would argue that China’s exchange rate (against
both the dollar and the basket) has many similarities
to the old world of fixed but adjustable currencies.
True, it moves on a controlled path when it moves

### Page 12

12
between different levels rather than jumping up and
down. But the PBOC does frequently target a level,
and that targeted level does change over time.
I would further argue that steps like the recent tariff
rate quota that will limit China’s exports of hybrid
cars to Europe to around 350,000 units next year (up
from 2025, but well below the 800,000 units of
2026) aren’t going to address the underlying
imbalance on their own. They can provide a bit of a

buffer for specific sectors.
But that buffer, while necessary, won’t address the
broad-based erosion of manufacturing capacity
outside China. This erosion will persist as long as an
undervalued RMB sends the entire world a signal to
use China as its production base.
The new system of managed trade that is emerging in
autos would need to be progressively expanded to an
ever-increasing set of sectors if protection is used as a
substitute for both domestic reform in China and

exchange rate adjustment.
That is now the direction of travel, but it doesn’t
address the underlying systemic issues.
Incidentally, those who believe the path of internal
demand is critical to rebalancing and that exchange
rate moves are secondary need to explain how an
acceleration in domestic demand would, on its own,

### Page 13

13
lead China’s external accounts to adjust. One
important, stylized fact about China is that domestic
demand growth is no longer feeding over to import
growth (ex-gold, that is).
Bottom line: China, which needs a 1.5 percentage
point contribution from net exports every year, is on a
collision course with its trading partners without a
currency correction that provides the basis for more
balanced trade even in the absence of trade
restrictions.
And there isn’t a realistic path towards balance that

doesn’t involve a stronger RMB; there aren’t, as Paul
Krugman once put it, immaculate transfers.
* That downturn shows up in domestic deflationary
pressure, and weak job growth/high rates of (at least
youth) unemployment. China, in other words, isn’t in
“internal balance.” I really liked the report from

### Page 14

14
Kamakshya Trivedi and Hui Shan of Goldman Sachs
arguing China needs two tools to hit two targets
(internal and external balance).
** To be sure, rate differentials matter for exporter
conversion—but so does the expected movement of
the RMB. The market understands this better than
the Fund. The Chen and Suwanapruti (Goldman
Sachs) note uses a framework similar to mine to
describe the CNY’s moves in Q3:
“In our view, sizeable FX inflows have continued to support

RMB appreciation despite unfavorable interest rate
differentials, as strong export receipts and increased exporter
FX conversions have outweighed portfolio outflows. Our
estimated FX conversion ratio rose to 63% in August,
compared with around 45% in normal years, likely reflecting a
shift in market expectations from RMB depreciation to
appreciation.”
*** Š ef č ovi č  has jurisdiction over trade policy
(narrowly defined), but not broader macroeconomic

policies. Europe’s institutional architecture for
exchange rate coordination is not well-developed.
**** The current account surplus is no longer masked
to the same extent as in 2023 and 2024, as SAFE has
fiddled with its BoP methodology once again (per the
Federal Reserve) and the BoP goods surplus is no
longer significantly below the customs goods surplus.
Of course, there are still problems in the data—the
deficit in the investment income account most

米国中間選挙は、 大統領任期4年の中間である2年目に行われる連邦議会選挙です。 2026年11月3日に実施され、 下

院の全435議席と上院の約3分の1が改選対象となります。 政権に対する 「中間評価」 と しての意味合いを持ち、 今後の
政策運営に大きな影響を与えます。
本選に先立って行われた各党の候補者選び （予備選挙） では、 トランプ大統領の後押しを受けた共和党候補が相次い
で勝ち上がった一方、 民主党でも現職政権への対決姿勢を鮮明にする候補が選ばれるなど、 両党ともに熱心な支持層
の意向が強く出た結果となりました。 しかし本選に向けては、 高止まりする物価や生活費負担への不満を背景に トランプ
大統領の支持率が低下傾向にあると報じ られるなど、 政権側には逆風も吹いています。 有権者の間では、 長引く中東情
勢の悪化を受けたインフレや雇用環境といった 「生活防衛」 が最大の投票判断基準となっているほか、 国境管理 ・ 不法移
民問題や人工妊娠中絶の権利といった社会的テーマも、 勝敗の鍵を握る激戦区 （スイング ・ ステー ト） での浮動票の行方
を左右する重要な争点です。
執筆時点の情勢と しては、 上院は共和党優位、 下院は接戦ながら民主党がやや有利とみられています。 先行きへの影

響と しては、 共和党が上下両院の過半数を維持する 「 ト リプルレ ッ ド」 が続けば、 トランプ政権の政策実行力が高まり規制
緩和などが進みやすくなる一方、 関税引き上げや財政悪化への懸念が残ります。 反対に、 民主党が下院を制して 「ねじれ
議会」 となった場合は、 法案や予算が通りにく くなり政権運営が停滞する反面、 過度な強硬策にブレーキがかかる点がか
えって安心材料と受け止められる側面もあります。 中間選挙の結果は、 米国の国内政治にとどまらず、 今後の通商政策
や金利の先行きを見極める分岐点となります。 （主席エコノミス ト ：  阿原健一郎）

## Document 510: グラフで見る関西経済（2026年10月）

Source PDF: all report pdf/グラフで見る関西経済（2026年10月）.pdf
Report source: MURC

### Page 1

経済調査
グラフで見る関西経済
（2026年10月）
2026年10月13日
調査部 主任研究員

企業景況感（日銀短観）
日銀短観9月調査の業況判断DI（「良い」－「悪い」）は、全産業で＋21ptと6月調査（＋17ｐｔ）から改善。製
造業、非製造業ともに改善した。業種別に見ると、製造業では化学、非鉄金属、はん用・生産用・業務用機
械などが、非製造業は建設、物品賃貸、対個人サービスなどが改善している。先行き（12月）については、
製造業、非製造業ともに悪化が見込まれている。

マインド・景況感
9月の消費者態度指数（季節調整値）は34.7と3ヵ月連続で上昇した。

50を21ヵ月連続で下回った。秋物需要や連休・インバウンドで人出が増加したものの、物価高による消費
マインドの低下から景況感が悪化した。

## Document 520: フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 ～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引_efcf5aff

Source PDF: all report pdf/フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 ～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引_efcf5aff.pdf
Report source: DLRI
Date: 2026-10-22
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Asia Trends

2026 年10 月 22 日

1/2 
フィリピン中銀、原油高とペソ安の「二重苦」に直面し、大幅利上げに舵 
～インフレ圧力の高まりを受けて利上げ幅を拡大、金融引き締めの長期化も～ 
 経済調査部 主席エコノミスト  西濵 徹 
（℡：050-5474-7495）

（要旨） 
○ フィリピン中銀は、10 月 22 日の定例会合で政策金利を50bp 引き上げ、5.50％とすることを
決定した。４会合連続の利上げとなり、今回は利上げ幅を拡大するなど、金融引き締めの度
合いを強化した。背景には、イラン情勢の悪化に伴う原油高や、金融市場における通貨ペソ
安の進行を受けてインフレ圧力が強まったことがある。９月のインフレ率は前年比＋7.2％と
中銀の目標を大幅に上回っている。中銀はインフレ率が 2027 年も高水準で推移し、2028 年
に目標中央値の３％に近付くとの見通しを示した。レモロナ総裁は、今回の利上げが為替安
定を目的とするものではないと強調する一方、追加利上げや緊急利上げの可能性を排除しな
い姿勢を示した。先行きもイラン情勢の不透明感やペソ安圧力が続く可能性があり、さらな
る金融引き締めを迫られる展開も予想される。

フィリピン中央銀行は、10 月 22 日に開催した定例の金融政策委員会において、政策金利である
翌日物リバースレポ金利を 50bp 引き上げ、5.50％とすることを決定した。同行は、４月の定例会
合で約２年半ぶりの利上げに舵を切り、その後も６月、 ８月の定例会合で追加利上げを実施してお
り、今回で４会合連続の利上げとなる。さらに、今回は利上げ幅を拡大させるなど金融引き締めの
度合いを強めており、政策金利も2025 年６月以来の高い水準となった。 
背景には、イラン情勢の悪化による原
油高が世界的なエネルギーインフレを招
くなか、同国は原油需要の９割以上を輸
入に依存しており、その影響に晒されや
すいことがある。直近９月のインフレ率
は前年比＋7.2％と中銀の目標レンジ （２
～４％）を大きく上回っている（図１） 。
さらに、原油などエネルギー資源価格の
高止まりは輸入額の増加を通じて対外収
支を悪化させており、金融市場では通貨
ペソの対ドル相場が最安値を更新するな
図 1 インフレ率の推移

（出所）CEIC より第一ライフ資産運用経済研究所作成

コアインフレ率 インフレ率

### Page 2

本資料は情報提供を目的として作成されたものであり、投資勧誘を目的としたものではありません。作成時点で、第一ライフ資産運用
経済研究所が信ずるに足ると判断した情報に基づき作成していますが、その正確性、完全性に対する責任は負いません。見通しは予告
なく変更されることがあります。また、記載された内容は、第一生命保険ないしはその関連会社の投資方針と常に整合的であるとは限
りません。 
2/2 
ど、輸入物価の押し上げがインフレ圧力を一段と増幅させる懸念も高まっている。このため、中銀
としては物価安定に向けた『より強力な』対応を示す必要があるとの判断から、今回は市場予想を
上回る大幅利上げに舵を切ったと考えられる。 
会合後に公表した声明文では、 先行きの物価動向について 「今後数ヵ月は一段の加速が見込まれ
る」としたうえで、 「インフレ率は2027 年も高水準で推移し、2028 年には目標の中央値 （３％） に
近付く」 との見通しを示した。 さらに、 「コアインフレ率も上昇が予想され、 潜在的なインフレ圧力
の高まりを示唆している」 との見方を示した。 その理由として、 「イラン情勢の再燃による原油価格
の上昇」 、 「交通運賃の上昇によるインフレの上振れリスクが高まっている」ほか、 「エルニーニョ
現象による農業生産や価格へのリスク」を挙げた。一方で、景気動向について「先行きは財政支援
による回復が見込まれる」との見通しを示した。 
今回の決定については 「インフレ圧力の高まりと、 さらなる上振れリスクに対して強力な政策対
応の必要性を認識した」との考えを示した。先行きについて「今回の対応が金融環境を適切に抑制
的なものにする」 、 「副次的な影響を含めてインフレ期待をしっかりと抑える」 との見通しを示した。
また、先行きの政策運営については「物価安定という主要任務に基づいて、インフレ率を３％に戻
すべくあらゆる措置を講じる用意がある」 と過去数会合と同じ文言を載せており、 追加利上げに含
みを持たせた。 
会合後の記者会見に臨んだレモロナ総裁は、 物価見通しについて 「これまでの想定に比べて悪い」
との見方を示した。今回の決定を受けた政策スタンスについて「抑制的であるが、十分に抑制的で
あるかは定かでない」 と追加利上げに含
みを持たせた。 今回の利上げについて
「ペソ相場の安定を目的としたもので
はない」 、 「われわれは多少為替介入を実
施しているが、 相場の乱高下を防ぐこと
を目的としたもの」として、為替安定の
ための利上げではないと付言した。 とは
いえ、 「ペソ安はインフレを招く恐れが
あり、懸念している」と述べるなど、そ
の動向を注視する姿勢をみせた （図２） 。
先行きの政策運営について 「さらなる利
上げの可能性を排除しない」、「利上げ幅については 25bp か 50bp、それ以上かは言えない」としつ
つ、 「緊急利上げの必要性も議論された」 とした。こうした姿勢は、 中銀が切迫した状況に直面して
いることを示唆している。イラン情勢は見通しが立ちにくく、ペソ相場についても下落圧力に晒さ
れる展開が続く可能性があり、その動向には注意が必要である。 
以 上

## Document 524: ベッセント長官が日本の金融・財政政策を気にする理由

Source PDF: all report pdf/ベッセント長官が日本の金融・財政政策を気にする理由.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-20
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 20 日

調査レポート

ベッセント長官が日本の金融・財政政策を気にする理由

～米国の国債市場が抱える潜在リスクと日本への示唆

調査部 研究主幹 中塚伸幸

〇米国のベッセント財務長官が日本の金融･財政政策に「注文」をつける背景には、日本の政策動向が米国の
国債市場に悪影響を及ぼしかねないとの懸念がある。 
〇そもそも米国の連邦政府の赤字は GDP の 6%程度で高止まりしており、フローの観点だけをとれば日本より
状況は厳しい。特に、利払費が歳出の約 14%を占め、大きな負担になっている。財政赤字に加えて、米国債
市場には調達の短期化、ヘッジファンドの保有拡大、民間起債との競合といったリスク要因もある。 
〇日本もいずれ利払費の増加に直面することが見込まれ、ベッセントの「正論」は傾聴に値するといえよう。

1. ベッセント長官は米国債市場への影響を懸念し、日本の政策に「注文」 
米国のベッセント財務長官は 9 月の G20 財務相・中央銀行総裁会議後の会見で「日本はリフレ政策をやめる
べきだ」と発言した。また、これに先立つ日銀･植田総裁との会談では「円安に対処するための断固とした市場･金
融政策を支持する」と伝えた、と米･財務省が公表している。さらに、ニューヨークタイムズ紙は、ベッセント長官が
5 月の片山財務大臣との会食の場で「日銀が利上げすれば円安を是正できる」と大臣に伝えたと報じている。 
米国政府が日本の経済政策に言及・干渉することは珍しいことではないが、ベッセント長官は日本の金融･財
政政策に「注文」をつけている。これは、「米国債のトップセールスマン」を自認する財務長官が、日本の政策動向
が米国債市場に悪影響を及ぼすことを強く懸念しているためと考えられる。それだけ米国の国債市場は不安定
化リスクを内包しているということであり、実際、10 年債の金利は年初の 4.2%程度から足元は 5.2%程度まで上昇
している。日本が円安是正のために米国債を売却するといったことは避けてもらいたく、日本はまずは自らの金
融・財政政策によって円安に対処せよ、ということであろう。 
ベッセントが日本の政策を気にする背景にある、米国債市場のリスク要因について、以下に整理する。 
2. 米国の連邦政府赤字は GDP 比 6%、日本よりも状況は厳しい 
米国の財政状況は厳しい。今月公表された CBO の速報値では、2026 会計年度（25 年 10 月～26 年 9 月、
以下、「年度」）の連邦政府赤字は約 2 兆ドル（1 ドル＝158 円換算で 316 兆円）と、前年比で 12%増加した（次
ページ図表 1）。名目 GDP に対する比率では、26 年度を含めここ数年 6%程度の高水準が続いている。歳出は
パンデミック対応で 20 年度と 21 年度に急増したあと、22 年度以降は縮小したが、24 年度以降は再び増加が続
く。歳入も増加してはいるが、歳出増には及ばず、赤字が拡大している（図表 2）。

### Page 2

歳入･歳出の定義が異なるため日米の比較は難しいが、米国に近づけるために日本の一般会計の歳入から公
債金と前年度剰余金受入を控除し、歳出から債務償還金を控除して収支尻を試算すると、24 年度決算ベースで
日本の中央政府（一般会計）の赤字幅はGDP の 3%程度とみられ、かつ、足もとで比率は改善傾向にある。米国
は GDP の 6%という高水準の赤字が続き、改善の兆しも見られず、日本以上に厳しい状況といえるだろう。

日米の中央政府の歳入・歳出構造の違いとしては、米･連邦政府の収支には日本では社会保障基金に相当
する収支が含まれる点もある。具体的には、歳入にはペイロール税（日本の社会保険料に相当）が含まれ、歳出
にはソーシャル･セキュリティ･ベネフィット（老齢年金給付等）、メディケア（高齢者医療保険給付等）などが含まれ
る。したがって、より平仄を合わせる形で比較するには、中央政府に地方政府と社会保障基金も加えた「一般政
府」ベースで日米を比較することが適切である。この点、IMF の直近の World Economic Outlook Database に
基づいて一般政府ベースの財政収支とグロスの政府債務を比較すると（図表3、4）、政府債務の GDP 比率は日
本が圧倒的に高い一方で、GDP 比でみた財政赤字は米国のほうが日本より厳しい状況にあるといえる。

### Page 3

3. 米国は利払い負担が極めて大きい  
財政収支の悪化は主要先進国に共通する傾向であるが、特に米国の状況が厳しい理由として、利払費負担
が非常に大きいことがある。22 年以降の長期金利上昇に伴って利払費は急増しており、25 年度の利払費（Net 
interest）は歳出全体の 14%を占め、国防費よりもウエイトが高くなっている（図表 5）。利払費は 22 年度から 24
年度まで 3 年連続して前年比 30%以上の伸びであり、25 年度も 10%増、26 年度は若干定義が異なる Net 
interest on public debt の速報ベースで 11%増と、依然として高い伸びが続く。 
 日本の一般会計の利払費（決算ベース）は 24 年度まで 7～8 兆円でほとんど横ばいであるのに対し、米･連邦
政府歳出の利払費は急激に増加しており、大きな違いがある（図表6）。これは、日本の場合は超低金利が長く続
き、その間に調達した債券のウエイトが高いため、債券残高で加重平均した実効金利が低い（ 25 年度末の加重
平均金利は 0.98%）のに対し、米国では金利水準自体が日本より高いうえに、22 年以降急激に長期金利が上昇
したことが影響している（米国債の加重平均金利は 26 年 9 月末で約 3.5%）。 
 ただし、日本の場合も、 10 年債の市場金利はすでに 3%程度まで上昇しており、今後、これに伴う利払費の増
加は避けられない。24 年度までの決算ベースでは図表 6 のようにほぼ横ばいであるが、当初予算ベースでは利
払費は 24 年度の 9.7 兆円から 26 年度には 13.0 兆円へと 2 年間で 3 割以上増加しており、借り換えの進展に
伴ってさらに増加していくものと見込まれる。

### Page 4

4. 法人減税が歳入の伸びを抑制  
 米･連邦政府の歳入は相応に増加している。特に、歳入全体の半分を占める個人所得税は 25 年度に前年比
9%増、26 年度も同 7%増と、着実に伸びている。一方、法人所得税は、歳入全体に占めるウエイトは1 割以下で
あるが、トランプ政権による減税（OBBB 法による一部設備投資の即時償却の恒久化等）により、26 年度は前年
比で 16%も減少している（25 年度も、24 年度には災害等による前年の延期納付分があったことを調整した上で
も、やはり減少）。即時償却によって投資が促進され、将来的に税収増となる可能性はあるが、少なくとも足元はト
ランプ政権の減税政策が歳入を抑えている面は否めない（図表 7、8）。なお、関税も、相互関税が違法とされ還
付となったことから、26 年度は前年比 11%の減少となった。

5. 米国債市場の潜在リスク ～期間の短期化とヘッジファンドによる保有の拡大  
 上述の財政収支そのものの悪化に加えて、米国の国債市場には潜在リスクとなる構造的特徴がある。一つは、
調達が相対的に短期化しており、借り換え対応のプレッシャーが相応に高まっていることである。米国債の平均
残存期間は 5 年 11 カ月と日本の普通国債の平均残存期間 9 年 5 カ月に比べて短い。また、米国債のうち 1 年
以内に償還される債券の比率は 26 年 9 月時点で 33%と、19 年の 27%から上昇しており（次ページ図表 9）、日
本の普通国債の 13%と比べても比率は高い。IMF も、こうした調達短期化の潜在的なリスクを今年 4 月の 4 条
報告で指摘している1。 
 もう一つはヘッジファンドによる保有が拡大していることで、状況によってはそれが市場を不安定化させる懸念
があると指摘されている。FRB は今年 6 月の論文で、国債全体のうちヘッジファンドが保有する比率は 23 年の
約 4.5%から 25 年 9 月には約 8.5%に拡大しており、ヘッジファンドはレバレッジをかけた裁定取引が多いため、
何らかのショックが起きた場合に市場の混乱を増幅させる可能性がある、と警鐘を鳴らしている2。

1 IMF, “United States: 2026 Article IV Consultation,” April 2026 
2 Phillip J. Monin, “Decomposing Hedge Funds’ U.S. Treasury Exposures,” FEDS Notes, June 22, 2026

### Page 5

6. 民間の投資適格社債との競合  
 米国債市場に影響を与える要因としては、民間の投資適格債の起債が増加していることも挙げられる。ハイパ
ースケーラーによるデータセンター建設資金の調達などにより、投資適格社債の発行額は 24 年以降に急増して
おり、26 年も第 3 四半期までの累計額で前年比 25%増と、すでに 25 年通年の実績と同水準に届いている（図
表 10）。こうした投資適格債は比較的安全な資産として米国債と競合するため、国債の需給を緩ませる要因にな
り得る。実際、FRB のウォーシュ議長は 9 月 FOMC 後の会見で、長期金利上昇の要因として、強い景気、地政
学的要因とともに、「資金調達の競争（competition for capital）」を挙げている。

7. 日本への示唆 ～「正論」は傾聴に値する  
 ベッセント長官の「注文」は、内政干渉ともいえるが、しかし正論である。背景にある米国の状況を整理したが、
米国側の事情を抜きにしても、日銀の利上げと財政規律の維持は円安是正の点から日本に必要なことと思われ
る。特に、財政運営においては、足もとはインフレによる税収増に加えて、過去の低利調達の恩恵に負う部分が
大きく、現状約 1%の実効金利はいずれ 3%程度に上昇する。そうなれば、利払費は今よりも 20 兆円膨らむ。さら
に、防衛費や社会保障費の拡大などが見込まれる中、来年度からの食品消費税減税の財源も明らかになってい
ない。ベッセント長官の「助言」は真摯に受け止めるべきであろう。

---
## Document 530: ユーロ圏ＧＤＰ（2026年7-9月期）－主要4か国はいずれも前期比伸び率が低下

Source PDF: all report pdf/ユーロ圏ＧＤＰ（2026年7-9月期）－主要4か国はいずれも前期比伸び率が低下.pdf
Report source: NLI
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 2

### Page 1

1． 結果の概要：前期比 0.4％と高成長を記録 
10月30日、欧州委員会統計局（Eurostat）はユーロ圏ＧＤＰの一次速報値（Preliminary Flash 
Estimate）を公表し、結果は以下の通りとなった。

・前年同期比は1.3％、市場予想（1.1％）を上回り、前期（1.2％）から上昇した

2． 結果の詳細：アイルランド要因を除くと前期比伸び率はやや減速 
ユーロ圏の26年 7-9月期の成長率は前期比0.4％（年率換算 1.7％）となった。4-6月期（前期
比 0.6％、年率 2.6％）から減速したが、高めの成長率を維持した。前年同期比の伸び率は 1.3％
（4-6月期 1.2％）だった。なお、近年はアイルランドがユーロ圏全体の成長率も大きく左右して
おり、アイルランドを除くユーロ圏の実質成長率（図表 1）は 7-9月期が前期比 0.2％（年率換算
0.8％） 、前年同期比 1.2％、4-6月期が前期比0.3％（年率換算 1.2％） 、前年同期比 1.3％であり、
成長基調は維持されているものの前期比での伸び率はやや減速している。 
経済規模の大きい4か国の伸び率を見ると、前期比ではドイツ 0.2％（4-6月期 0.3％）、フラン
ス▲0.1％（同0.0％）、イタリア0.0％（同 0.2％）、スペイン0.6％（同0.7％）となり（図表3横
軸）、これまで同様スペインが高成長を維持しているが、いずれの国でも成長率は低下した。特に足
もとではフランスの伸び悩みが目立つ。前期比伸び率について、ドイツ統計局は投資が減少する一

## Document 585: 凱基投顧_1590 亞德克-KY_莊政翰_20261029

Source PDF: all report pdf/凱基投顧_1590 亞德克-KY_莊政翰_20261029.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 8
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

十月 29, 2026 研究網站: https://investment.kgisia.com.tw/Portal/Report/Index/Zh/R  Powered by the EFA Platform 1

動態更新 工業自動化 ‧台灣

亞德客-KY 
(1590.TW /1590 TT)

營收穩健增長 
重要訊息 
亞德客- KY 3Q26 每股盈餘 14.23 元，大致符合預期，主因較高稅負抵銷了
營業利益優於預期的表現。管理層預期 4Q26 營收將季持平，優於過往 季節
性，並看好 2027 年營收將維持雙位數成長，動能來自 氣動產品在電子、汽
車及電池應用增長 ，以及線性滑軌貢獻提升。  
評論及分析 
3Q26 營業利益優於預期。 3Q26 營收 115 億元，年增率擴大至 40%，分別
較市場共識及凱基預估高出 4%及 13%，因中國政府刺激政策推動下，氣動
元件市場規模擴大，公司市佔 率同步提升，加上線軌業務改善。儘管面臨原
料成本上升壓力，公司憑藉產能滿載與生產效率 提升，帶動毛利率年增 3.3
個百分點至 49.4%，優於市場和凱基預估的 49.2%、48.2%。加上營運槓桿
效益，營業利益穩健年增 59%至 39 億元，較市場共識及我們預估分別高出
5%及 13%。然而，公司為 發放股利而匯回海外盈餘需負擔額外 10%所得
稅，推升有效稅率，使 3Q26 EPS 為 14.23 元，大致符合凱基預估的 14.33
元，對比市場共識為 15.01 元。 
2027 年營收可望維持雙位數成長。亞德客-KY 3Q26 主要氣動元件終端應用
年增 15%-35%，並預期在中國政府智慧製造與產業升級措施支持下 ，電
子、汽車、工具機及一般機械等應用在 2026 年、甚至 2027 年，可維持雙位
數成長。線性滑軌業務表現亦亮眼，年初迄 今營收年增 27%。公司預期線軌
產能利用率將由目前 40%年底進一步提升至 50%，屆時毛利率可望達
30%。展望 2027 年，公司預估線軌營收將年增逾 20%，加上氣動元件持續
擴大市佔率，看好整體營收維持雙位數成長。  
新產品佈局進展順利。除線軌業務持續改善外，公司表示毛利率高達 60%的
電控產品年初迄今營收已成長逾 20%。此外，半導體應用新產品亦取得良好
進展，今年月營收已由 2025 年的人民幣 500 萬元提升至人民幣 1,300 萬
元。受惠中國政府持續推動半導體產業在地化生產與採購，相關產品 2026
年營收可望突破人民幣 1 億元，2027 年進一步提升至人民幣 1.5 億元以上。
公司維持 2026 年營收年增逾 20%、營益率 34%的目標不變，我們認為半導
體新產品與線軌業務將挹注 2027 年營收潛在上檔。  
投資建議  
基於產業上升週期可望延續至 2027 年，我們認為亞德客-KY 目前 18 倍本益
比的評價具吸引力 (歷史交易區間為 15-35 倍)，反映獲利成長以及未來股利
配發率提升的潛力。維持「增加持股」評等 及目標價 1,885 元。 
投資風險  
下檔風險包括中國需求 走緩和線軌營收貢獻下滑。

圖 3: 公司概況  圖 4: 電子設備業為主要營收來源 
成立於 1989 年，亞德客 -KY 為中國第二大的氣動元件廠商，
市佔率預估為 29%，僅次於日商 SMC 的 34%。公司主要生產
氣動執行元件  (氣缸)、氣動控制元件  (閥類) 及氣源處理元件，
並以自有品牌亞德客銷售。亞德客-KY 在 1995 年進入中國市
場，2025 年中國營收貢獻約 94%，海外市場 4%，台灣營收
佔比為 2%。
